# OPP_internal=[] | AlphaGate={pass=True,est=45,thresh=30} |
# Tranche={action=ENTER,level=1} | Mode=NORMAL | Quant=REAL(momentum) |
# DRL=EXIT_ONLY | Intent={asset=BTC/USD,dir=+0.80,exp=5.0%}
if [ -n "$LATEST_LOG" ]; then
    echo "Last 3 proof entries (raw):"
    grep "^\[PROOF\]" "$LATEST_LOG" | tail -3
    echo ""
    
    echo "Alpha gate pass rate:"
    ALPHA_PASS=$(grep -c "AlphaGate={pass=True" "$LATEST_LOG" 2>/dev/null || echo "0")
    ALPHA_FAIL=$(grep -c "AlphaGate={pass=False" "$LATEST_LOG" 2>/dev/null || echo "0")
    ALPHA_TOTAL=$((ALPHA_PASS + ALPHA_FAIL))
    if [ "$ALPHA_TOTAL" -gt 0 ]; then
        echo "  Pass: $ALPHA_PASS / $ALPHA_TOTAL ($(( ALPHA_PASS * 100 / ALPHA_TOTAL ))%)"
    else
        echo "  No alpha gate entries yet"
    fi
fi

# 1. Process
PID=$(pgrep -f "[m]ain.py.*paper" 2>/dev/null)
echo "1. Process:        $([ -n "$PID" ] && echo "✅ RUNNING (PID $PID)" || echo "❌ NOT RUNNING")"

# 2. Ticks
if [ -n "$LATEST_LOG" ]; then
    PROOFS=$(grep -c "^\[PROOF\]" "$LATEST_LOG" 2>/dev/null || echo "0")
else
    PROOFS=0
fi
echo "2. Proof entries:  $([ "$PROOFS" -gt 0 ] && echo "✅ $PROOFS" || echo "❌ 0 — no ticks processed")"

# 3. Crashes
if [ -n "$LATEST_LOG" ]; then
    CRASHES=$(grep -c "TICK_FATAL" "$LATEST_LOG" 2>/dev/null || echo "0")
else
    CRASHES=0
fi
echo "3. Fatal crashes:  $([ "$CRASHES" -eq 0 ] && echo "✅ 0" || echo "❌ $CRASHES")"

# 4. P0 Safety
if [ -n "$LATEST_LOG" ]; then
    P0_STUBS=$(grep -E "RiskManager.*STUB|P0SafetyIntegrator.*STUB|ExistenceFuse.*STUB|ThesisBudgetGovernor.*STUB" "$LATEST_LOG" | wc -l)
else
    P0_STUBS=-1
fi
echo "4. P0 Safety:      $([ "$P0_STUBS" -eq 0 ] && echo "✅ All P0 ACTIVE" || echo "❌ $P0_STUBS P0 components STUB")"

# 5. Regime
if [ -n "$LATEST_LOG" ]; then
    REGIME_UNKNOWN=$(grep -c "regime=UNKNOWN" "$LATEST_LOG" 2>/dev/null || echo "0")
    REGIME_TOTAL=$(grep -c "regime=" "$LATEST_LOG" 2>/dev/null || echo "1")
fi
echo "5. Regime:         $([ "$REGIME_UNKNOWN" -lt "$((REGIME_TOTAL / 2))" ] && echo "✅ GMM active" || echo "⚠️ $REGIME_UNKNOWN/$REGIME_TOTAL UNKNOWN")"

# 6. Sentiment
if [ -n "$LATEST_LOG" ]; then
    SENT=$(grep -c "\[SENTIMENT_L1\]" "$LATEST_LOG" 2>/dev/null || echo "0")
else
    SENT=0
fi
echo "6. Sentiment:      $([ "$SENT" -gt 0 ] && echo "✅ $SENT entries" || echo "❌ No F&G data")"

# 7. Funding
if [ -n "$LATEST_LOG" ]; then
    FUND=$(grep -c "\[COINGLASS\]" "$LATEST_LOG" 2>/dev/null || echo "0")
else
    FUND=0
fi
echo "7. Funding rate:   $([ "$FUND" -gt 0 ] && echo "✅ $FUND entries" || echo "⚠️ No Coinglass data")"

# 8. Alpha gate
if [ -n "$LATEST_LOG" ]; then
    A_PASS=$(grep -c "AlphaGate={pass=True" "$LATEST_LOG" 2>/dev/null || echo "0")
    A_FAIL=$(grep -c "AlphaGate={pass=False" "$LATEST_LOG" 2>/dev/null || echo "0")
else
    A_PASS=0; A_FAIL=0
fi
A_TOT=$((A_PASS + A_FAIL))
echo "8. Alpha gate:     $([ "$A_TOT" -gt 0 ] && echo "✅ $A_PASS/$A_TOT pass ($((A_PASS * 100 / (A_TOT > 0 ? A_TOT : 1)))%)" || echo "⚠️ No data")"

# 9. Trades
if [ -n "$LATEST_LOG" ]; then
    TRADES=$(grep -c "\[PAPER_PNL\]" "$LATEST_LOG" 2>/dev/null || echo "0")
else
    TRADES=0

# 10. Existence Fuse

python        # =================================================================
        # Phase 1.10: MAX HOLD TIME CHECK
        # Force-exit positions that exceed max_hold_hours for their mode
        # NORMAL=72h, OPPORTUNITY=168h
        #
        # FIX: Execute inline instead of returning early, because execution
        # only happens at STEP 7 (~line 3420) and early returns bypass it.
        # Also use target_exposure=0 to trigger Branch A (FULL EXIT) in the
        # paper position tracker, not Branch C (flip to opposite direction).
        # =================================================================
        if asset in self._position_entry_times:
            entry_info = self._position_entry_times[asset]
            held_hours = (datetime.utcnow() - entry_info["entry_time"]).total_seconds() / 3600
            max_hours = self._max_hold_hours.get(entry_info["mode"], 72)
            if held_hours > max_hours:
                current_exposure = market_data.get("current_exposure", 0.0)
                if abs(current_exposure) > 0.001:
                    logger.warning(
                        f"[MAX_HOLD_TIMEOUT] {asset} held {held_hours:.1f}h > "
                        f"{max_hours}h ({entry_info['mode']}) — EXECUTING forced exit"

                        execution_mode=ExecutionMode.AGGRESSIVE,

                    try:

                        logger.info(
                            f"[MAX_HOLD_TIMEOUT] {asset} exit executed: {exec_result.get('status', 'UNKNOWN')}"
                        )
                    except Exception as e:
                        logger.error(f"[MAX_HOLD_TIMEOUT] {asset} exit FAILED: {e}")

                    # Clean up entry time tracking
                    self._position_entry_times.pop(asset, None)

                    # Save positions after forced exit
                    self._save_paper_positions()

                    # Return non-actionable intent — tick is done for this asset
                    return TradeIntentV36(asset=asset)
                else:
                    # No position but entry tracked — cleanup stale entry
                    self._position_entry_times.pop(asset, None)
Key Changes Explained

target_exposure=0.0 instead of abs(current_exposure):

Paper tracker line 4069: is_full_exit = (intent.target_exposure == 0 or intent.direction == 0)
With target_exposure=0, this is TRUE → Branch A (FULL EXIT) fires
Old code: target_exposure=abs(current_exposure) → is_full_exit=FALSE → falls to Branch C (flip) → opens new opposite position


await self._execute_intent() inline instead of return:

Execution happens right here, not at STEP 7
The intent is actually sent to the execution manager and paper position tracker


self._position_entry_times.pop() explicit cleanup:

Old code relied on line 4478 (intent.target_exposure == 0) to clean up, but that only runs inside _execute_intent → paper tracker
Belt-and-suspenders: clean up here too


Returns TradeIntentV36(asset=asset) (empty non-actionable):

Tick is done — we don't want the engine to produce another decision
The regular v36 path would be confusing after we already force-closed

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - HMATS v6.7: Hierarchical Multi-Agent Trading System for SOL/ETH/BTC on Kraken
   - 4H master tick cycle with `_process_4h_tick_inner()` as the monolithic ~4500-line function
   - Execution only happens at STEP 7 (~line 3420) — any `return` before that bypasses execution
   - Paper position tracker branches: Branch A (FULL EXIT, target_exposure=0), Branch B (PARTIAL EXIT, same direction + shrinking notional), Branch C (FLIP, direction change)
   - `is_actionable` = `not veto_active AND abs(direction) > 0.1 AND target_exposure > 0.01`
   - TradeIntentV36: represents desired state (not delta), re-submitted every tick
   - `notional_usd` recalculated from `base_quantity × current_price` each tick — price drift causes tiny deltas
   - Existence fuse: 28-day window, -5% PnL threshold, manual recovery. Has `to_dict()`/`from_dict()` methods
   - `account_sync._dry_run_pnl` accumulates PnL during runtime but was never persisted
   - Regime power multipliers: QUIET_ACCUMULATION=0.8, VOLATILE_CHOP=1.5, etc.
   - PORTFOLIO_CAP: strategic coordinator soft cap with 20% tolerance
   - Shadow ledger: JSONL daily files with INTENT/FILL/POSITION triplets
   - Tranche escalation: T1→T4 with increasing exposure targets

     max_hold_intent = TradeIntentV36(
         asset=asset,
         direction=exit_direction,
         target_exposure=0.0,  # FIX: 0 triggers Branch A (FULL EXIT)
         veto_active=False,
         force_execution=True,
         execution_mode=ExecutionMode.AGGRESSIVE,
         urgency=1.5,
     )
     # Execute immediately (don't return early — bypasses STEP 7)
     try:
         exec_result = await self._execute_intent(asset, max_hold_intent, market_data)
         logger.info(f"[MAX_HOLD_TIMEOUT] {asset} exit executed: {exec_result.get('status', 'UNKNOWN')}")
     except Exception as e:
         logger.error(f"[MAX_HOLD_TIMEOUT] {asset} exit FAILED: {e}")
     self._position_entry_times.pop(asset, None)
     self._save_paper_positions()
     return TradeIntentV36()
     ```

     **Bug 3 Fix (lines 3976-3990): Exposure delta guard at STEP 7**
     - Root cause: system re-submits full target state every tick; price drift creates $2-5 notional deltas
     - Added guard before STEP 7 execution check:
     ```python
     _current_exp = market_data.get("current_exposure", 0.0)
     _exp_delta = abs(intent.target_exposure - abs(_current_exp))
     if (intent.is_actionable and not intent.veto_active
             and _exp_delta < 0.005 and abs(_current_exp) > 0.001
             and not getattr(intent, 'force_execution', False)):
         logger.debug(
             f"[STEP7_SKIP] {asset}: exposure delta {_exp_delta:.4f} < 0.005 "
             f"(target={intent.target_exposure:.4f}, current={abs(_current_exp):.4f}) — skipping execution"
         )
         intent.veto_active = True
         intent.veto_reason = "EXPOSURE_DELTA_BELOW_THRESHOLD"
     ```

Fix 1: orderbook_depth NaN → Tick Killed [P1, 30 min]
Root Cause
main.py line 5399: orderbook_depth_1pct = float("inf") — safe default when no orderbook data.
main.py line 5911: raw['orderbook_depth'] = min(depth_1pct / _depth_median, 5.0)
When depth_1pct = inf fills the rolling buffer, median([inf, inf, inf]) = inf,
and inf / inf = NaN.
integration_v36.py line 773: validate_market_data() checks for NaN via
value != value → fails → entire tick vetoed.
Result: 9 ticks killed by NaN in orderbook_depth. Each killed tick means
no trading for that asset for 4 hours.
Fix
File: main.py, around line 5899-5912:
Find:
python        depth_1pct = raw.get('orderbook_depth_1pct_usd', 500000)
        _asset_key = asset.upper().replace("/USD", "")
        _depth_buf = self._depth_history.get(_asset_key)
        if _depth_buf is not None and depth_1pct > 0:
            _depth_buf.append(depth_1pct)
Replace with:
python        depth_1pct = raw.get('orderbook_depth_1pct_usd', 500000)
        _asset_key = asset.upper().replace("/USD", "")
        _depth_buf = self._depth_history.get(_asset_key)
        # FIX: Don't feed inf into rolling buffer — it poisons median → NaN
        if _depth_buf is not None and depth_1pct > 0 and depth_1pct < float('inf'):
            _depth_buf.append(depth_1pct)
Also add a NaN guard at the output:
Find:
python        raw['orderbook_depth'] = min(depth_1pct / _depth_median, 5.0) if _depth_median > 0 else 1.0
Replace with:
python        _raw_depth = min(depth_1pct / _depth_median, 5.0) if (_depth_median > 0 and depth_1pct < float('inf')) else 1.0
        import math
        raw['orderbook_depth'] = _raw_depth if (not math.isnan(_raw_depth) and not math.isinf(_raw_depth)) else 1.0
Verification
bashgrep "NaN value in field: orderbook_depth" logs/*.log | wc -l

# T7: RiskManager.daily_pnl 永远是 0.0，每日亏损限制是空壳

## 问题

`risk/risk_manager.py` L178: `self.daily_pnl: float = 0.0`

`daily_pnl` 只在 `close_position()` (L479) 中被更新：
```python
def close_position(self, symbol, exit_price):
    ...
    self.daily_pnl += pnl
```

但 `main.py` 从不调用 `close_position()`。main.py 的三个 risk_manager 调用是：
- L1008: `initialize(initial_capital)`
- L2959: `check_trade_allowed(...)` ← 这里检查 daily_pnl，永远通过
- L4508: `update_balance(new_equity)`

仓位生命周期完全在 `self._paper_positions` (main.py) 中管理，与 `self.risk_manager.positions` 完全解耦。

`check_trade_allowed()` L259:
```python
if self.current_balance > 0 and self.daily_pnl <= -self.current_balance * self.config.max_daily_loss:
    return False, f"Max daily loss reached ({self.config.max_daily_loss:.1%})"
```
因为 `daily_pnl` 永远是 0.0，这个检查永远通过。**每日亏损限制是个摆设。**

## 改动

### 1. `risk/risk_manager.py` — 在 `_reset_daily_counters` 附近加一个轻量方法

```python
def record_realized_pnl(self, pnl: float) -> None:
    """Record realized PnL from a closed position. Updates daily_pnl for loss limit checks."""
    self.daily_pnl += pnl
    self.daily_trades += 1
    logger.info(f"[RISK] Realized PnL: ${pnl:+,.2f} | daily_pnl=${self.daily_pnl:+,.2f} | trades={self.daily_trades}")
```

### 2. `main.py` — 在仓位关闭后调用

在 ~L4100 附近，`pnl_usd` 计算之后，`shadow ledger` 记录之前，加：

找到这段（大约 L4098-4102）：
```python
                        pnl_pct = (exit_price - entry_price) / entry_price * pos_dir
                        pnl_usd = pnl_pct * pos_notional
                        if self.account_sync and self.account_sync.dry_run:
                            self.account_sync.update_dry_run_pnl(pnl_usd)
```

在 `account_sync.update_dry_run_pnl` 之后加：
```python
                        # Feed realized PnL to RiskManager for daily loss limit tracking
                        if self.risk_manager:
                            self.risk_manager.record_realized_pnl(pnl_usd)
```

## 验证

```bash
grep -n "record_realized_pnl" main.py risk/risk_manager.py
# main.py 应有 1 处调用，risk_manager.py 应有方法定义
```

- 关一笔 -$100 的仓 → `daily_pnl` 变 -100
- 再关一笔 -$200 → `daily_pnl` 变 -300
- 如果 balance=$10K, max_daily_loss=0.03 → 阈值 $300 → 下一笔交易被拒
- `_reset_daily_counters()` 在新一天时归零（已有逻辑 L517-527）

- 不要修改 `close_position()` 的签名

     **Fix 3 (NAV/Drawdown logging, lines 5658-5696 + 6372):**
     ```python
     # Periodic NAV/Drawdown snapshot (every round)
     try:
         current_equity = 100_000.0
         if self.account_sync:
             try:
                 current_equity = self.account_sync.get_equity()
             except Exception:
                 pass
         if not hasattr(self, '_peak_equity'):
             self._peak_equity = current_equity
         self._peak_equity = max(self._peak_equity, current_equity)
         drawdown_pct = 0.0
         if self._peak_equity > 0:
             drawdown_pct = (self._peak_equity - current_equity) / self._peak_equity
         self._current_drawdown_pct = drawdown_pct
         logger.info(
             f"[NAV] equity=${current_equity:,.2f} | "
             f"peak=${self._peak_equity:,.2f} | "
             f"drawdown={drawdown_pct:.2%} | "
             f"positions={len(self._paper_positions)} | "
             f"round={round_count}"
         )
         if drawdown_pct >= self.config.hard_drawdown_halt:
             logger.critical(
                 f"[DRAWDOWN_HALT] Drawdown {drawdown_pct:.2%} >= "
                 f"halt threshold {self.config.hard_drawdown_halt:.2%} — "
                 f"STOPPING PAPER TRADING"
             )
             self._running = False
     except Exception as e:
         logger.debug(f"[NAV] Snapshot failed: {e}")
     ```

         self.daily_pnl += pnl
         self.daily_trades += 1
         self.logger.info(f"[RISK] Realized PnL: ${pnl:+,.2f} | daily_pnl=${self.daily_pnl:+,.2f} | trades={self.daily_trades}")

问题根因： Dashboard 读 shadow ledger 的 POSITION 记录优先于 paper_positions.json。BTC 在 Round 2 产生了方向翻转 决策（shadow ledger 记录 new_direction=+1），但实际仓位没变（paper_positions.json 仍然 SHORT @ $67,848）。Dashboard 展示了 决策意图 而不是 实际仓位。
为什么 Entry=0, Notional=0： Shadow ledger 的 POSITION 记录只有方向变更，没有 fill 数据。Dashboard 尝试从配套的 FILL 记录读 entry_price，但 FILL 记录不存在（因为实际没成交）。
修复方向： Dashboard 的 load_positions() 应该交叉验证——当 shadow ledger 和 paper_positions.json 矛盾时，信任 paper_positions.json（那才是实际持仓状态）。或者更简单：直接用 paper_positions.json 作为 primary source，shadow ledger 只补充 fill 细节。

# HMATS 全系统策略与特殊设计 — 子组件级完整审计清单 V3

> **来源**: Project 全历史对话深度回溯 (v1 → v8+)
> **角度**: 子组件粒度 (每个子系统拆解到阈值 / 算法 / 数据流)
> **V2→V3 变化**: 73 项扩展为 110+ 子组件级条目, 补充所有阈值/公式/状态机
> **标注**: 🆕V3 = 本次新增子组件级展开

---

## 一、Alpha 生成层 (11 大项 → 36 子组件)

### 1. Quant Agent: 3×4 策略矩阵

(完整策略列表见 V2，此处聚焦子组件)

#### 1a. 🆕V3 IR-Softmax 策略选择器

```
选择机制:
  每个 4H tick → 4 策略独立评估 → 生成 raw_score
  regime_adjusted_score = raw_score × regime_fitness_multiplier
  winner = argmax(regime_adjusted_score)  ← winner-take-all, 非平均

IR-Softmax 权重:
  w_i = exp(IR_i / τ) / Σ exp(IR_j / τ)
  τ = 0.5 (温度参数)
  IR = annualized(mean(returns) / std(returns))
  
Regime Fitness 乘数表 (Production v6+):
  PANIC_SELLOFF:     momentum ×1.5, liquidation ×1.5, breakout ×0.8, mean_revert ×0.5
  MOMENTUM_RALLY:    momentum ×1.3, breakout ×1.1, mean_revert ×0.6, liquidation ×0.7
  QUIET_ACCUMULATION: mean_revert ×1.3, momentum ×0.8, breakout ×0.7, liquidation ×0.5
  WEAK_CONSOLIDATION: mean_revert ×1.2, momentum ×0.9, breakout ×0.8, liquidation ×0.6
  EXTREME_VOLATILITY:  breakout ×1.2, momentum ×1.0, mean_revert ×0.7, liquidation ×1.0
```

#### 1b. 🆕V3 单策略内部信号链 (以 momentum 为例)

```
输入特征:
  RSI(14), MACD(12,26,9), EMA_cross(9,21), ADX(14), Volume_ratio(7d)

信号生成:
  direction = sign(macd_histogram) × (1 if ema9 > ema21 else -1)
  strength = clip(adx/100, 0, 1) × volume_ratio_norm
  confidence = min(rsi_distance_from_50/50, 1.0) × adx_norm
  
出口:
  → QuantSignal(direction, strength, confidence, strategy_name="momentum")
  → 进入 regime_fitness 加权 → winner-take-all
```

### 2. DRL Agent: TQC + FiLM Position A

(核心参数见 V2，此处展开训练/推理子组件)

#### 2a. 🆕V3 FiLM Extractor 架构细节

```
FiLM Position A ("条件化 LSTM 输入"):
  
  输入: observation (126 dim) + regime_embedding (8 dim)
  
  LSTM 前 FiLM 层:
    γ = Linear(regime_embed, hidden_dim) + 1.0  ← 初始化为恒等
    β = Linear(regime_embed, hidden_dim) + 0.0  ← 初始化为零
    conditioned_input = γ ⊙ lstm_input + β
  
  LSTM: input_dim=hidden_dim, hidden=256, layers=2
  
  输出: 256 dim → policy_head + value_head

参数量: ~166K (vs FiLM Full 4.2M → 过拟合)
训练初始化: γ=1, β=0 → 训练开始时 FiLM = 恒等变换, 渐进学习 regime 条件化
```

#### 2b. 🆕V3 TQC_SAFE 配置 (防梯度爆炸)

```
关键超参:
  ent_coef = 0.1              ← 固定! "auto" 在高波动市场不稳定 → 梯度爆炸
  learning_rate = 3e-5        ← 低 LR 更稳定 (原 1e-4 爆炸)
  buffer_size = 2_000_000     ← 1M→2M 后稳定性显著提升
  batch_size = 512
  gradient_steps = 4
  n_quantiles = 25
  top_quantiles_to_drop = 2   ← 截断高分位数 → 悲观估计 → 保守行为
  target_entropy = -1.0
  
奖励函数 (Classic PnL):
  R = 0.50 × pnl_reward
    + 0.25 × profit_retention_reward
    + 0.15 × tranche_efficiency_reward
    + 0.10 × exit_timing_reward
  
  reward_clip = 20            ← Stage 8 Optuna 优选
  
方向-Regime 对齐奖励:
  熊市做空: +1.2× 乘数
  牛市做多: +1.1× 乘数
  牛市扛空: -0.03 惩罚/step
  熊市扛多: -0.05 惩罚/step (更重)
```

#### 2c. 🆕V3 DRL 权威与降权机制

```
DRL 当前角色: EXIT_ONLY
  → 只能建议 "增加退出压力" / "释放 runner"
  → 不能决定入场方向或仓位大小
  
升级路径: EXIT_ONLY → FULL authority
  条件: StatisticalPromotionGate
    - 30 天 shadow mode 数据
    - shadow 决策 vs 实际决策对比
    - 统计显著性检验通过
    
DRL 自动降权条件:
  entropy > 0.8 → 不可信, Quant 接管
  连续 N 次与市场方向相反 → confidence 衰减
  regime 不稳定期 → 强制 ADVISE only
```

### 6. 🆕V3 Binance Lead-Lag Alpha Engine (完整数据流)

```
┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│              BINANCE → KRAKEN LEAD-LAG PIPELINE              │
├──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│                                                              │
│  数据源: Binance Perpetuals WebSocket (aggTrade stream)      │
│  ├── BTC: wss://fstream.binance.com/ws/btcusdt@aggTrade     │
│  ├── ETH: wss://fstream.binance.com/ws/ethusdt@aggTrade     │
│  └── SOL: wss://fstream.binance.com/ws/solusdt@aggTrade     │
│                                                              │
│  BinanceTakerMonitor:                                        │
│  ├── 实时聚合: buy_volume_1s, sell_volume_1s                 │
│  ├── imbalance_1s = (buy - sell) / (buy + sell)             │
│  ├── 均值/标准差: EMA(α=0.01) 在线更新                       │
│  ├── zscore = (volume_1s - mean) / std                      │
│  └── spike_detected = |zscore| > 3.0σ                       │
│                                                              │
│  信号生成 (get_lead_signal):                                 │
│  ├── 前提: spike_detected = True                             │
│  ├── latency_ms = (now - binance_timestamp) × 1000          │
│  ├── half_life_remaining = max(0, 200ms - latency_ms)       │
│  ├── strength = |taker_signal| × (half_life / 200ms)        │
│  ├── DVOL boost: if dvol.spike_alert → strength × 1.5       │
│  └── status: VALID (<200ms) / STALE (200-400ms) / INVALID   │
│                                                              │
│  信号衰减公式:                                                │
│  signal_strength = divergence × volume_zscore × e^(-t/200ms) │
│                                                              │
│  执行建议:                                                    │
│  ├── strength < 0.3 → WAIT                                   │
│  ├── signal > +0.3  → BUY (on Kraken)                       │
│  ├── signal < -0.3  → SELL (on Kraken)                      │
│  └── urgency = strength × (half_life_remaining / 200ms)     │
│                                                              │
│  安全约束:                                                    │
│  ├── latency > 400ms → 信号作废 (防止追逐 ghost moves)       │
│  ├── Binance vs Kraken 价差 > 1% → NO_TRADE 触发             │
│  └── Lead-Lag 只有 3 个权力:                                  │
│      1. TRIGGER Opportunity Mode                              │
│      2. 设置 Execution Mode (PASSIVE/AGGRESSIVE)             │
│      3. 提供 Flow Evidence                                    │
│      × 不能直接修改 target exposure                           │
└──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

### 7. 🆕V3 Deterministic Sentiment Engine (v6.4.1b)

```
设计哲学: 相同输入 → 相同输出 (可重现, 非 LLM)

Pipeline:
  1. 数据源抓取
     ├── CoinGecko API → Fear/Greed Index
     ├── LunarCrush → social_volume, galaxy_score
     └── Funding Rate 聚合 → market_sentiment_proxy

  2. Keyword Matching + Rule Engine
     ├── bullish_keywords: ["breakout", "moon", "accumulation", ...]
     ├── bearish_keywords: ["crash", "dump", "regulation", ...]
     └── rule: direction_score = (bull_count - bear_count) / total

  3. 输出
     ├── direction_score: [-1, +1]
     ├── strength: confidence 度量
     └── crowding_bias (来自 LunarCrush): 
         extreme_crowding → soft penalty (×0.7), 永远不 veto

关键约束:
  - LunarCrush 只用于 crowding_bias, 不用于方向判断
  - extreme_crowding = soft penalty, 永远不 veto
  - sentiment 永远不做 multiplier, 只能 TRIGGER / VETO / MODE_SWITCH
  - Extreme Sentiment Shock > 3σ → VETO 相反方向
```

## 二、微观结构层 — VPIN / OFI / Vol-of-Vol (🆕V3 全新分类)

### 10. 🆕V3 GPU VPIN Calculator (Toxic Flow 检测核心)

```
算法: Easley, López de Prado, O'Hara (2012)
      Volume-Synchronized Probability of Informed Trading

原理:
  将交易量分桶 (volume bucket), 计算每桶内 buy/sell 不平衡
  VPIN = Σ|V_buy - V_sell| / (n × V_bucket)
  高 VPIN = 知情交易者占比高 = 逆向选择风险高

实现:
  ├── bucket_size = 50,000 (交易量/桶)
  ├── num_buckets = 50
  ├── lookback_buckets = 50
  ├── GPU加速: CuPy 向量化 (RTX 5090)
  └── 延迟: ~0.5ms

Trade 分类 (Tick Rule + Bulk Classification):
  price > prev_price → BUY
  price < prev_price → SELL
  price == prev_price → 50/50 split

VPIN Z-score:
  zscore = (current_vpin - mean(vpin_history)) / std(vpin_history)
  需要 >= 20 个历史数据点

毒性分级 (ToxicityLevel):
  ┌─────────────┬────────┬──────────────────────┐
  │ 级别         │ VPIN   │ 含义                  │
  ├─────────────┼────────┼──────────────────────┤
  │ SAFE        │ < 0.30 │ 正常市场, 知情交易少   │
  │ NORMAL      │ 0.30~  │ 轻微升高              │
  │ ELEVATED    │ 0.50~  │ 知情交易占比显著       │
  │ TOXIC       │ 0.70~  │ 高逆向选择风险         │
  │ EXTREME     │ ≥ 0.85 │ 极端, 几乎全是知情交易 │
  └─────────────┴────────┴──────────────────────┘
```

---

### 11. 🆕V3 VPINToxicityFilter (Toxic Flow Veto 执行层)

```
┌───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│            VPIN TOXIC FLOW VETO — 完整决策链                  │
├───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│                                                               │
│  输入: vpin (float), urgency (float, 0-1)                    │
│                                                               │
│  紧急度调整:                                                   │
│    adjusted_threshold = base_threshold + (urgency - 0.5) × 0.1│
│    (高紧急度 → 稍微放松阈值, 允许在稍高毒性下执行)            │
│                                                               │
│  决策矩阵 (VPINToxicityFilter.filter()):                     │
│  ┌──────────────┬───────────┬───────────────┬────────────┐   │
│  │ VPIN 区间     │ 动作       │ size 乘数     │ 执行模式    │   │
│  ├──────────────┼───────────┼───────────────┼────────────┤   │
│  │ < 0.50       │ PROCEED   │ 1.0 (100%)    │ 任意       │   │
│  │ 0.50 ~ 0.70  │ REDUCE    │ 0.5 (50%)     │ PASSIVE    │   │
│  │ 0.70 ~ 0.85  │ DELAY     │ 0.25 (25%)    │ PASSIVE    │   │
│  │ ≥ 0.85       │ ABORT     │ 0.0 (禁止)    │ ABORT      │   │
│  └──────────────┴───────────┴───────────────┴────────────┘   │
│                                                               │
│  毒性阻尼 (Signal Fusion 层):                                 │
│  ┌──────────────┬──────────────────┐                         │
│  │ SAFE         │ dampening = 1.0  │                         │
│  │ CAUTION      │ dampening = 0.8  │                         │
│  │ ELEVATED     │ dampening = 0.6  │                         │
│  │ TOXIC        │ dampening = 0.3  │                         │
│  └──────────────┴──────────────────┘                         │
│  final_signal = raw_signal × toxicity_dampening              │
│                                                               │
│  执行模式选择 (AdvancedSignalFusion):                         │
│  ┌──────────────────────┬─────────────────┐                  │
│  │ 条件                  │ 执行模式         │                  │
│  ├──────────────────────┼─────────────────┤                  │
│  │ VPIN TOXIC           │ ABORT           │                  │
│  │ VPIN ELEVATED        │ PASSIVE         │                  │
│  │ |signal| > 0.8       │ AGGRESSIVE      │                  │
│  │ |signal| > 0.5       │ TWAP            │                  │
│  │ else                 │ PASSIVE         │                  │
│  └──────────────────────┴─────────────────┘                  │
│                                                               │
│  Fusion 层权重偏移 (VPIN → 偏向 Quant 保守):                  │
│  ├── TOXIC:     w_quant += 0.30                              │
│  ├── ELEVATED:  w_quant += 0.15                              │
│  └── 逻辑: 毒性高 = 市场微结构恶化 → 信任 Quant 而非 DRL      │
│                                                               │
│  统计追踪:                                                    │
│  ├── mean_vpin, max_vpin (滚动窗口)                           │
│  ├── toxic_rate = count(vpin > 0.85) / total                 │
│  └── action_counts: {PROCEED: n, REDUCE: n, DELAY: n, ABORT: n}│
│                                                               │
│  在 Tranche Pyramiding 中的作用:                               │
│  T3 (30%) 入场条件: 必须无 VPIN spike                         │
│  SOL Dominance 退出: VPIN > 0.80 → 触发退出                   │
└───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

### 12. 🆕V3 GPU OFI Calculator (Order Flow Imbalance)

```
算法: 订单簿变化追踪买卖压力

计算逻辑:
  每次 orderbook snapshot:
    Bid side:
      bid_price > prev_bid_price → ofi += bid_size    (新买单)
      bid_price == prev_bid_price → ofi += (bid_size - prev_bid_size)
      bid_price < prev_bid_price → ofi -= prev_bid_size (买单撤退)
    
    Ask side:
      ask_price < prev_ask_price → ofi -= ask_size    (新卖单)
      ask_price == prev_ask_price → ofi -= (ask_size - prev_ask_size)
      ask_price > prev_ask_price → ofi += prev_ask_size (卖单撤退)

累积 OFI: cumulative_ofi += ofi_update
动量: momentum = sum(ofi_history[-10:])

压力判定:
  zscore > +2.0 → BUY_PRESSURE
  zscore < -2.0 → SELL_PRESSURE
  else → NEUTRAL

用途:
  ├── 在 QuantSignal 生成中: 确认方向 (OFI 与信号同向 → 加分)
  ├── 在执行层: 大买压 → 买入时用更激进模式
  └── 在 Lead-Lag: OFI 与 Binance taker 同向 → 信号增强
```

---

### 13. 🆕V3 GPU Orderbook Analyzer (深度分析)

```
GPU 加速 L2 订单簿分析 (RTX 5090):

指标计算:
  imbalance = (bid_volume - ask_volume) / (bid_volume + ask_volume)
  spread_bps = (best_ask - best_bid) / mid_price × 10000
  vwap_bid = Σ(bid_price × bid_qty) / Σ(bid_qty)
  vwap_ask = Σ(ask_price × ask_qty) / Σ(ask_qty)
  depth_1pct = total_volume within 1% of mid_price
  depth_2pct = total_volume within 2% of mid_price

大单检测:
  large_bids = Σ(bid_qty where bid_qty > 3 × mean_bid_qty)
  large_asks = Σ(ask_qty where ask_qty > 3 × mean_ask_qty)

NO_TRADE 触发:
  Liquidity < $100K within 1% → NO_TRADE (Category B: Systemic Risk)

执行滑点预估:
  estimate_fill_price(quantity, side) → (avg_price, levels_consumed)
  遍历 orderbook 层级, 累加直到数量满足
```

---

### 14. 🆕V3 Vol-of-Vol Calculator

```
波动率的波动率 — Regime 状态检测

计算:
  returns = log(price[t] / price[t-1])
  realized_vol = std(returns, window=20)
  vol_of_vol = std(realized_vol, window=20) / mean(realized_vol)

Z-score:
  vov_zscore = (vol_of_vol - mean(vov_history)) / std(vov_history)

Regime 分类:
  CALM:      vov_zscore < -1.0
  NORMAL:    -1.0 <= vov_zscore < 1.0
  ELEVATED:  1.0 <= vov_zscore < 2.0
  EXPLOSIVE: vov_zscore >= 2.0

融合影响:
  EXPLOSIVE → w_quant += 0.20 (偏向保守的 Quant)
  
Cross-asset 相关性:
  correlation_btc_eth = rolling_corr(btc_returns, eth_returns, 30d)
  correlation_btc_sol = rolling_corr(btc_returns, sol_returns, 30d)
  Correlation > 0.92 → NO_TRADE 触发 (Category B: Systemic Risk)
```

## 三、巨鲸追踪系统 (🆕V3 全新专题)

### 15. 🆕V3 WhaleTracker (核心追踪器)

```
┌───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│                    WHALE TRACKER 完整架构                      │
├───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│                                                               │
│  阈值配置:                                                    │
│    WHALE_THRESHOLD_SOL = 10,000 SOL (约 $1.5M+)              │
│    whale_outflow_threshold_usd = $50,000,000 ($50M)          │
│    whale_transfer_threshold_sol = 1,000 SOL                  │
│    large_inflow_threshold_sol = 50,000 SOL                   │
│    whale_threshold_usd = $1,000,000 ($1M = 算鲸鱼)           │
│                                                               │
│  已知 CEX 地址库 (SOL):                                       │
│    Binance Hot:  9WzDXwBbmPdCBoccYRV...                      │
│    Kraken Hot:   5tzFkiKscXHK5ZXCGbX...                      │
│    Coinbase Hot: H8sMJSCQxfKiFTCfDR3...                      │
│    OKX Hot:      GdnSyH3YtwcxFvQrVVJ...                      │
│    (生产环境从环境变量/配置加载)                                │
│                                                               │
│  转账分类逻辑:                                                 │
│    from_is_cex AND NOT to_is_cex:                             │
│      → CEX_WITHDRAWAL (提现 = 买入信号 = INFLOW)             │
│      → buy_volume += amount                                  │
│    to_is_cex AND NOT from_is_cex:                             │
│      → CEX_DEPOSIT (充值 = 卖出信号 = OUTFLOW)               │
│      → sell_volume += amount                                 │
│    两者都不是:                                                 │
│      → LARGE_TRANSFER (中性)                                  │
│                                                               │
│  影响评分 (Impact Score):                                     │
│    impact = min(1.0, log(1 + amount/threshold) / log(100))   │
│    使用对数比例, 避免极大值主导                                │
│                                                               │
│  鲸鱼压力指标 (Whale Pressure):                               │
│    pressure = (buy_vol - sell_vol) / (buy_vol + sell_vol)    │
│    范围 [-1, +1]:                                             │
│      +1 = 全是买入 (CEX 提现, 看涨)                          │
│       0 = 平衡                                                │
│      -1 = 全是卖出 (CEX 充值, 看跌)                          │
│    lookback: 默认 60 分钟滚动窗口                             │
│                                                               │
│  WhaleAlert 数据结构:                                         │
│    alert_type: CEX_WITHDRAWAL / CEX_DEPOSIT / LARGE_TRANSFER │
│                / DEX_SWAP / SMART_MONEY_MOVE                  │
│    address, amount_sol, direction (INFLOW/OUTFLOW/NEUTRAL)    │
│    tx_signature, address_label, counterparty                  │
│    price_at_time, value_usd, impact_score                    │
│    timestamp                                                  │
│                                                               │
│  缓存: max_alerts = 1000 (deque)                             │
│  线程安全: threading.RLock                                    │
└───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

### 18. 🆕V3 FlowSignalIntegrator (统一资金流信号)

```
整合所有流量信号为统一交易信号:

FlowState 数据结构:
  ├── whale_direction/confidence/net_flow_usd, whale_alert
  ├── exchange_direction/confidence/net_flow_usd
  ├── btc_etf_direction/eth_etf_direction/etf_confidence/streak_days
  ├── sol_network_health (0-1), jito_pressure (0-1)
  └── combined_direction/confidence

权重配置:
  whale_weight    = 0.25
  exchange_weight = 0.25
  etf_weight      = 0.30
  network_weight  = 0.20

鲸鱼信号转换:
  |net_whale_flow_usd| > $1M:
    direction = +1.0 if flow > 0 (net 买入), -1.0 if flow < 0 (net 卖出)
    confidence = min(|flow| / ($1M × 5), 1.0)
    whale_alert = True

交易所流信号 (反向!):
  |net_exchange_flow_usd| > $10M:
    direction = -1.0 if flow > 0 (流入交易所 = 卖出压力)
    direction = +1.0 if flow < 0 (流出交易所 = 买入信号)

ETF 流信号:
  consecutive_outflow_days >= 3 → etf_outflow_caution = True

数据过时处理:
  stale_data_seconds = 300 (5分钟)
  超时 → confidence 衰减

Mock Mode:
  mock_mode = True (默认, 真实数据源不可用时使用合成数据)
```

### 19. 🆕V3 OnChainInflowMonitor (Kraken SOL 充值监控)

```
专门监控 Kraken SOL 充值地址:

充值地址来源 (优先级):
  1. config.kraken_deposit_addresses
  2. 环境变量 KRAKEN_SOL_DEPOSIT_ADDRESSES
  3. is_exchange_deposit 标志 (fallback)

转账记录:
  record_transfer(amount_sol, from_addr, to_addr, is_exchange_deposit):
    if 目标是 Kraken → exchange_inflow += amount
    if 来源是 Kraken → exchange_outflow += amount
    net_flow = inflow - outflow
    
    大额转账: amount >= whale_transfer_threshold (1000 SOL) → large_transfers++
    极大充值: amount >= large_inflow_threshold (50K SOL) → whale_alert!

压力检测:
  get_inflow_pressure() → (is_elevated, net_flow_sol)
  is_elevated = net_flow >= 50,000 SOL

SOL Dominance 退出触发:
  exchange inflow spike → sell bias override → 退出 SOL 多头
```

### 21. 🆕V3 JitoTipsTracker (MEV 活动检测)

```
Jito Tips = Solana 上的 MEV 贿赂费

指标:
  avg_tip_lamports:      EMA 平均 tip
  tip_90th_percentile:   P90 tip
  bundle_success_rate:   bundle 成功率
  landed_tips_per_minute: 每分钟已 land 的 tips 数

Spike 检测:
  current = tip_90th_percentile
  baseline = max(1000 lamports, avg_tip)
  multiplier = current / baseline
  is_spike = multiplier >= 3.0× (jito_tip_spike_multiplier)

高 Tips 含义:
  ├── MEV 活动激烈 → 套利者活跃 → 执行环境恶化
  ├── 可能有前跑交易 → 滑点增大
  └── 市场操纵风险上升

响应:
  tip_spike → SOLRiskLevel 升级 → 仓位乘数降低
  极端 spike → CRITICAL → 禁止新仓
```

### 22. 🆕V3 NetworkCongestionMonitor (Solana 网络健康)

```
网络状态 (NetworkStatus):
  HEALTHY → DEGRADED → CONGESTED → HALTED

阈值配置:
  ┌──────────────────┬──────────┬──────────┐
  │ 指标              │ Warning  │ Critical │
  ├──────────────────┼──────────┼──────────┤
  │ TPS              │ < 2,000  │ < 1,000  │
  │ Slot Time (ms)   │ > 500    │ > 800    │
  │ Skip Rate        │ > 5%     │ > 15%    │
  └──────────────────┴──────────┴──────────┘
  
  TPS < 100 → HALTED (网络几乎停止)

状态计算:
  任一 critical 条件 → CONGESTED
  任一 warning 条件 → DEGRADED
  全部正常 → HEALTHY

指标:
  tps, slot_time_ms, priority_fee_estimate
  compute_units_consumed, vote_transaction_pct, skip_rate
```

## 五、Short Bias Agent 五组件系统 (🆕V3 全新专题)

### 23. 🆕V3 ShortBiasAgent 完整架构

```
┌───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│              SHORT BIAS AGENT — 五组件做空检测                 │
├───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│                                                               │
│  硬性约束:                                                    │
│    ✅ direction: 永远 <= 0 (-1.0 或 0.0, 绝不为正)           │

│    RSI > 80 (parabolic_rsi_extreme)                          │
│    价格从高点开始回落                                         │
│  confidence = 0~0.90, score = mean(sub_scores)               │
│                                                               │
│  组件 #2: Extreme Funding (权重 25%)                         │
│  ──────────────────────                                       │
│  触发:                                                        │
│    funding_rate > 0.05% = 高                                 │
│    funding_rate > 0.10% = 极端 (extreme_threshold)           │
│    funding_rate 趋势上升 (24h window)                         │
│    OI 同时增长 > 10%                                          │
│  含义: 多头过度拥挤 → 空头反扑概率高                          │
│  confidence capped at 0.80                                   │
│                                                               │
│  组件 #3: Liquidation Cascade (权重 20%)                     │
│  ─────────────────────────                                    │
│  触发:                                                        │
│    long_liquidations > 均值 3× (liquidation_long_spike_ratio)│
│    清算不平衡: long_liq >> short_liq                          │
│    OI 在历史高位 (>= 10% 增长)                                │
│  含义: 高杠杆多头被清算 → 级联效应                            │
│  confidence capped at 0.85                                   │
│                                                               │
│  组件 #4: Extreme Greed (权重 15%)                           │
│  ────────────────────────                                     │
│  触发:                                                        │
│    Fear/Greed Index > 80% (greed_extreme_threshold)          │
│    social_sentiment > 0.70 (社交媒体极度乐观)                 │
│    持续高位 (24h avg > 0.70)                                  │
│  含义: 逆向指标, 群体贪婪 = 顶部信号                         │
│  confidence capped at 0.70                                   │
│                                                               │
│  组件 #5: Whale Outflow (权重 10%)                           │
│  ──────────────────────                                       │
│  触发:                                                        │
│    whale_flow < -$50M (大额流出/CEX充值)                      │
│    exchange_inflow > $50M                                     │
│  含义: Smart money 出货                                       │
│  whale score = min(1.0, |flow| / ($50M × 3))                │
│  exchange score = min(1.0, inflow / ($50M × 2))             │
│  confidence capped at 0.65                                   │
│                                                               │
│  综合信号:                                                    │
│    weighted_score = Σ(component_score × weight)              │
│    direction = -1.0 if weighted_score > threshold            │
│    confidence = min(weighted_score, max_confidence=0.85)     │
│    primary_trigger = 最高分的那个组件                          │
│    suggested_stop = 3%, max_exposure = 30%                   │
└───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

## 六、执行层子组件 (🆕V3 展开)

### 24. 🆕V3 Fill-Slope Monitor (逆向选择检测)

```
原理: 快速成交 = 你在和知情交易者对手方

FillSlopeMonitor:
  记录每笔成交: expected_price, fill_price, quantity, timestamp
  slippage_bps = (fill_price - expected_price) / expected_price × 10000

质量分级:
  avg_slippage < 1 bps  → EXCELLENT → 建议 INCREASE_SIZE
  avg_slippage < 5 bps  → GOOD     → CONTINUE
  avg_slippage < 15 bps → POOR     → REDUCE_SIZE
  avg_slippage ≥ 15 bps → TOXIC    → PAUSE

核心规则:
  50% SOL 仓位在 < 500ms 内成交 → 判定为 Adverse Selection → 暂停执行
  原因: 你的限价单被秒吃 = 对方知道价格要往你的方向走
```

---

### 25. 🆕V3 Passive-Aggressive Executor (执行模式切换)

```
默认: PASSIVE (Maker 限价单, 避免 26bps taker 费)

切换到 AGGRESSIVE (Taker) 的条件:
  ├── lead_lag_confidence > 0.8 (Binance 领先信号强)
  ├── VPIN toxic + need_urgent_exit (毒性 + 需紧急退出)
  └── binance_volume_spike > 3.0σ (Binance 巨量)

强制 PASSIVE 的条件:
  VPIN > 0.85 + NOT need_urgent_exit → PASSIVE_ONLY
  (毒性极端但不紧急 → 只挂单不吃单)

Taker 允许条件 (Constitution):
  仅在 VPIN Toxic 或 Lead-Lag 高置信度 下允许 Taker
  其他情况一律 Maker
```

---

### 26. 🆕V3 Minimum Edge Filter

```
硬性抑制层: 只在预期 alpha 足以覆盖摩擦时执行

公式:
  predicted_alpha > 3 × (fees + slippage)

Kraken Pro 费率:
  Maker: 0 bps (Pro 免费额度内, $10K/月)
  Taker: 26 bps

最低 edge 要求:
  Maker: α > 3 × (0 + ~2 bps slippage) = 6 bps
  Taker: α > 3 × (26 + ~10 bps slippage) = 108 bps

含义: 系统默认 Maker 的另一个原因 — Taker 需要 18× 更大的 edge
```

---

### 27. 🆕V3 Dynamic Slicer (ATR 自适应切片)

```
SOL Dynamic Slicing (1-min ATR):

  atr_ratio = current_1min_ATR / MA(ATR, 14)

  ┌───────────────┬────────────────────────────┐
  │ ATR Ratio     │ 切片策略                    │
  ├───────────────┼────────────────────────────┤
  │ < 1.0         │ 标准 TWAP (5 slices)       │
  │ 1.0 ~ 1.5    │ 标准 TWAP (5 slices)       │
  │ 1.5 ~ 2.0    │ DYNAMIC_SLICE (15 slices)  │
  │ 2.0 ~ 3.0    │ DYNAMIC_SLICE (20-30)      │
  │ > 3.0        │ ABORT 或 极小切片           │
  └───────────────┴────────────────────────────┘

VPIN 调整:
  slice_size = base_slice × (1 - vpin)
  VPIN 越高 → 切片越小 → 降低市场冲击
```

### 28. 🆕V3 RL Execution Agent (25 维状态空间, v5.0.5)

```
状态空间 (25 维):
  [0-4]:   归一化 bid depths (5 层)
  [5-9]:   归一化 ask depths (5 层)
  [10-14]: 层级 spreads (5 层) ← v5.0.5 新增
  [15]:    spread_bps (归一化)
  [16]:    toxicity (VPIN) [0,1]
  [17]:    urgency [0,1]
  [18]:    order_imbalance [-1,1]
  [19]:    depth_pressure [-1,1] ← v5.0.5 新增
  [20]:    time_remaining [0,1]
  [21]:    order_size_ratio ← v5.0.5 新增
  [22]:    levels_to_fill ← v5.0.5 新增
  [23]:    bid_depth_ratio ← v5.0.5 新增
  [24]:    spread_expansion ← v5.0.5 新增

动作空间 (7 动作):
  0: Passive (挂 best bid/ask)
  1: Improve 1 level (改善 1 tick)
  2: Improve 2 levels (改善 2 ticks)
  3: Market order (立即成交)
  4: Split (50/50 限价/市价)
  5: Adaptive Slice (按深度动态分) ← v5.0.5
  6: Iceberg (冰山单) ← v5.0.5

实现滑点模型 (Square Root Impact):
  slippage = base_slip × toxicity × impact × √(size / 100K)
  IS = slippage_bps + fee_bps
```

### 35. 🆕V3 Squeeze Protection (完整)

```
Short Squeeze 概率计算:

输入:
  ├── OI (Open Interest) — 持仓量
  ├── Funding Rate — 资金费率
  ├── Short Liquidations — 空头清算量
  └── CVD — Cumulative Volume Delta

计算:
  squeeze_prob = f(oi_zscore, funding_extreme_negative, short_liq_spike, cvd_buy_surge)

响应:
  high probability → 减仓 + 放宽止损
  含义: 空头被轧 → 快速上涨 → 保护现有空头仓位
```

### 36. GMM 六态分类 (V2 已有)

### 37. 🆕V3 Regime Phase Detector (IGNITION→EXHAUSTION 完整状态机)

```
在单个 regime 内部的阶段分类:

┌─────────────┬────────────────────────────┬──────────────────┐
│ Phase       │ 特征                        │ 交易行为          │
├─────────────┼────────────────────────────┼──────────────────┤

## 十、Engineering Defense 子组件 (展开)

### 38-39. Veto Chain / Core Safety Constants (V2 已有)

### 40. 🆕V3 Kraken Integrity Shield (CRC32 完整)

```
Kraken WS V2 CRC32 Orderbook 校验:

机制:
  Kraken 在每个 orderbook update 中附带 CRC32 checksum
  客户端独立计算本地 orderbook 的 CRC32
  比较: local_crc32 == server_crc32

失败处理:
  checksum mismatch → 
    1. 自动 full reset (清空本地 orderbook)
    2. 通过 REST API 重新拉取完整 orderbook
    3. 从 WS 重新增量更新
    
连接状态机:
  CONNECTED → DISCONNECTED → RECONNECTING → CONNECTED
  指数退避: 1s → 2s → 4s → 8s → ... → max 60s
  连续失败计数 → 超限 → 报警

通道分离:
  WS Hot Channel: 实时数据 (orderbook, trades)
  REST Escape Channel: 降级通道 (WS 不可用时)

当前状态: IMPORTED but NOT INSTANTIATED in main.py
```

---

### 41. 🆕V3 StartupReconciler (启动对账)

```
启动时位置对账:

流程:
  1. 读取本地 Shadow Ledger 状态
  2. 查询 Kraken exchange 实际持仓
  3. 比对: local_positions vs exchange_positions
  4. 不一致处理:
     ├── 本地有, 交易所无 → 清除本地 ghost position
     ├── 交易所有, 本地无 → 导入为 untracked position
     └── 数量不匹配 → 以交易所为准, 更新本地
  5. 日志报警: 所有不一致项

安全:
  不一致 → 阻止新开仓直到对账完成
  防止: 重启后双倍开仓 / 遗漏平仓

当前状态: IMPORTED but NOT INSTANTIATED in main.py
```

### 42. 🆕V3 Hot Restart + VRAM Guard

```
Hot Restart (崩溃恢复):
  checkpoint_freq = 500K steps (训练)
  运行时: 每 5 分钟 checkpoint
    保存: shadow_ledger state, equity curve, open positions
  崩溃恢复: 最多 5 分钟数据丢失

VRAM Guard (GPU 内存管理):
  监控 RTX 5090 VRAM (32GB) 使用
  高压力 → 释放非关键 GPU cache
  防止训练/推理 OOM 崩溃
  优先级: 推理 > 训练 (交易优先)
```

     def record_realized_pnl(self, pnl: float) -> None:
         """Record realized PnL from a closed position. Updates daily_pnl for loss limit checks."""
         self.daily_pnl += pnl
         self.daily_trades += 1
         self.logger.info(f"[RISK] Realized PnL: ${pnl:+,.2f} | daily_pnl=${self.daily_pnl:+,.2f} | trades={self.daily_trades}")

           """Load current positions — paper_positions.json is ground truth."""
           positions = {}
           paper_pos = self._load_paper_positions_fallback()
           records = self._latest_session_records()
           fill_by_asset: Dict[str, Dict] = {}
           for r in records:
               if r.get("entry_type") == "FILL":
                   fill_by_asset[r.get("asset", "")] = r
           for asset in ["BTC", "ETH", "SOL"]:
               symbol = f"{asset}/USD"
               pp = paper_pos.get(asset, {})
               pp_dir = pp.get("direction", 0)
               if pp_dir != 0:
                   side = "SHORT" if pp_dir < 0 else "LONG"
                   fill_rec = fill_by_asset.get(asset)
                   last_fill_price = fill_rec["data"].get("price", 0) if fill_rec else 0
                   last_fill_fee = fill_rec["data"].get("fee", 0) if fill_rec else 0
                   positions[symbol] = {
                       "size": abs(pp.get("exposure", 0)),
                       "side": side,
                       "entry_price": pp.get("entry_price", 0),
                       "unrealized_pnl": -last_fill_fee if last_fill_fee else 0,
                       "notional": pp.get("notional", 0),
                       "tranche": pp.get("tranche", 0),
                       "last_fill_price": last_fill_price,
                   }
               else:
                   positions[symbol] = {
                       "size": 0, "side": "FLAT", "entry_price": 0,
                       "unrealized_pnl": 0, "notional": 0, "tranche": 0,
                   }
           return positions

   添加预算检查:
   if self.opportunity_budget and system_mode == "OPPORTUNITY":
       budget_ok = self.opportunity_budget.can_open(trigger_type=trigger_source)
       if not budget_ok:
           logger.warning(f"[OPP_BUDGET] Budget exhausted, downgrading to NORMAL")
           system_mode = "NORMAL"  # 降级到 NORMAL 模式

4. OPPORTUNITY 关闭时释放预算:
   在 OPPORTUNITY 结束/过期处调用:
   if self.opportunity_budget:
       self.opportunity_budget.release(trigger_type=trigger_source)

验证:
- 启动后日志显示 "OpportunityBudgetGovernor: ACTIVE"
- 多个 OPPORTUNITY 同时触发时看到预算检查日志
- 预算耗尽时正确降级到 NORMAL

安全约束:
- 读取 OpportunityBudgetGovernor 的 API 签名确认 can_open/release 方法名
- 如果方法名不同，调整调用
- 预算阻止不能影响 NO_TRADE 优先级 (NO_TRADE > OPPORTUNITY > NORMAL)
```

   atr_ratio = market_data.get('atr_1m', 0) / market_data.get('atr_1m_avg', 1)

   elif atr_ratio < 2.0: n_slices = 15

- slice 数量不能为 0 或负数

## C1. VPIN 实时化 — Live 部署硬性前提 ⭐⭐⭐

```
背景:
当前 VPIN 在 paper mode 下 hardcoded 为 0.35:
- main.py L3925: vpin=market_data.get('vpin', 0.35)
- main.py L5460: "vpin": 0.35

VPIN 实时计算代码已存在:
- engine/compute/local_compute_engine.py L135: GPUVPINCalculator
- engine/compute/local_compute_engine.py L322: calculate_vpin()
- engine/compute/local_compute_engine_v31.py L106: VPINState

但 main.py 未导入或使用这些计算器。

任务:
1. 先读取 GPUVPINCalculator 的 API:
   sed -n '135,200p' engine/compute/local_compute_engine.py
   确认: calculate_vpin() 需要什么输入 (trade data? orderbook?)

2. 在 main.py 中导入 VPIN 计算器:
   VPIN_CALC_AVAILABLE = False
   try:
       from engine.compute.local_compute_engine import GPUVPINCalculator
       VPIN_CALC_AVAILABLE = True
   except ImportError as e:
       logger.warning(f"GPUVPINCalculator not available: {e}")

3. __init__ 中初始化:
   self.vpin_calculator = None
   if VPIN_CALC_AVAILABLE:
       self.vpin_calculator = GPUVPINCalculator()

4. 在 market_data 构建处 (~L5460 附近), 替换 hardcoded 值:
   if self.vpin_calculator:
       try:
           live_vpin = self.vpin_calculator.calculate_vpin(trade_data)
           market_data['vpin'] = live_vpin
       except:
           market_data['vpin'] = 0.35  # fallback

验证:
- market_data['vpin'] 值动态变化 (不再是固定 0.35)
- VPIN 值在 0-1 之间
- TOXIC (>0.70) 和 EXTREME (>0.85) 正确触发 VPIN 分级逻辑

安全约束:
- fallback 到 0.35 (中性值) 如果计算失败
- 不修改 VPIN 分级逻辑 (SAFE/ELEVATED/TOXIC/EXTREME)
- 确认 GPUVPINCalculator 是否需要 GPU, 如果需要但无 GPU, 用 CPU fallback
```

## P2. VPIN 实时计算替换 hardcoded 0.35

```
背景:
当前 VPIN 在 paper mode 下 hardcoded:
- main.py L3925: vpin=market_data.get('vpin', 0.35)
- main.py L5460: "vpin": 0.35

VPIN 实时计算代码已存在:
- engine/compute/local_compute_engine.py L135: GPUVPINCalculator
- engine/compute/local_compute_engine.py L322: calculate_vpin()
- engine/compute/local_compute_engine_v31.py L106: VPINState

但 main.py 未导入这些计算器。

任务:
1. 先读取 GPUVPINCalculator 和 VPINState 的 API:
   sed -n '135,200p' engine/compute/local_compute_engine.py
   sed -n '106,170p' engine/compute/local_compute_engine_v31.py
   确认: calculate_vpin() 需要什么输入? (trade data? tick data? orderbook?)

2. 确认是否需要 GPU。如果 GPUVPINCalculator 需要 CUDA:
   检查有无 CPU fallback 版本。
   engine/compute/local_compute_engine_v31.py 的 VPINState 可能是纯 CPU 版。

3. 在 main.py import 区域添加:
   VPIN_CALC_AVAILABLE = False
   try:
       from engine.compute.local_compute_engine_v31 import VPINState
       # 或 from engine.compute.local_compute_engine import GPUVPINCalculator
       VPIN_CALC_AVAILABLE = True
       logger.info("✓ VPIN Calculator loaded")
   except ImportError as e:
       logger.warning(f"VPIN Calculator not available: {e}")

4. __init__ 中初始化:
   self.vpin_calculator = None
   if VPIN_CALC_AVAILABLE:
       try:
           self.vpin_calculator = VPINState()  # 或 GPUVPINCalculator()
           logger.info("  VPIN Calculator: ACTIVE")
       except Exception as e:
           logger.warning(f"  VPIN Calculator: STUB ({e})")

5. 在 market_data 构建处 (~L5460), 替换 hardcoded:
   vpin_val = 0.35  # default fallback
   if self.vpin_calculator:
       try:
           # 根据实际 API 调用, 可能需要传入 trade_data/tick_data
           vpin_val = self.vpin_calculator.calculate_vpin(...)
       except Exception:
           pass  # keep fallback
   market_data['vpin'] = vpin_val

6. 如果 VPIN 计算需要 tick-level trade data 而 paper mode 没有:
   标记为 "VPIN 实时化需要 WebSocket tick feed, paper mode 暂无法实现"。
   在注释中说明: "Live 部署时从 Kraken WS 获取 tick data → 喂给 VPIN calculator"。

验证:
- 如果成功接入: market_data['vpin'] 不再是固定 0.35
- VPIN 值在 0-1 之间, 随市场变化
- TOXIC (>0.70) 和 EXTREME (>0.85) 正确触发分级逻辑

安全约束:
- 计算失败 fallback 到 0.35 (中性值)
- 不修改 VPIN 分级逻辑 (SAFE/ELEVATED/TOXIC/EXTREME)
```

# PRIORITY 3: DynamicSlicer — 执行质量提升
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

## P3. DynamicSlicer — 替换 num_slices=1 硬编码

```
背景:
当前 _execute_intent() 中 num_slices=1 (单笔执行)。
DynamicSlicer 概念代码分散在:
- orchestration/sota_v521_complete_integration.py L704-726 (切片逻辑)
- infra/unified_event_v521.py L390 (num_slices 参数)
没有独立 dynamic_slicer.py。

任务:
1. 找到 num_slices 硬编码位置:
   grep -n "num_slices.*=.*1\|n_slices.*=.*1" main.py
   
2. 读取 sota_v521 中的切片逻辑:
   sed -n '700,730p' orchestration/sota_v521_complete_integration.py
   
3. 在 _execute_intent() 中, 找到 num_slices 赋值处,
   替换为 ATR-based 动态计算:
   
   # ATR-based dynamic slicing
   atr_pct = market_data.get('atr_14_pct', 0.02)  # ATR as % of price
   if atr_pct < 0.01:      # Low vol
       num_slices = 3
   elif atr_pct < 0.02:    # Normal vol
       num_slices = 5
   elif atr_pct < 0.04:    # High vol
       num_slices = 10
   elif atr_pct < 0.06:    # Very high vol
       num_slices = 15
   else:                    # Extreme vol
       logger.warning(f"[DYN_SLICER] {asset}: ATR extreme ({atr_pct:.1%}), reducing to 3 slices + caution")
       num_slices = 3  # Fewer slices but larger, avoid extreme splitting
   
   # VPIN adjustment: toxic flow → smaller slices
   vpin = market_data.get('vpin', 0.35)
   if vpin > 0.70:
       num_slices = max(num_slices, 10)  # At least 10 slices in toxic flow
   
   logger.info(f"[DYN_SLICER] {asset}: slices={num_slices}, atr={atr_pct:.3%}, vpin={vpin:.2f}")

4. 如果 market_data 没有 atr_14_pct:
   检查 market_data 有什么 ATR 字段可用:
   grep -n "atr" main.py | grep market_data

验证:
- 日志显示 [DYN_SLICER] 行
- 低波动时 slices=3-5, 高波动时 slices=10-15
- num_slices 永远 ≥ 1

安全约束:
- num_slices 最小值 = 1 (不能为 0)
- 不改变总订单量, 只改变拆分方式
- 极端波动不 ABORT (paper mode 需要持续测试)
```

       elif _atr_ratio < 2.5:
           _num_slices = 10

           _num_slices = 15
       else:

## Analysis: CascadeExhaustionGovernor Integration

Based on my analysis of `risk/cascade_exhaustion_governor.py` and its current usage in the codebase, here are the findings:

---

### 1. **Public API**

**Class:** `CascadeExhaustionGovernor`

**Key Methods:**
- `update_metrics(liquidation_volume_1h, liquidation_volume_4h, price_change_1h_pct, price_change_4h_pct, volume_spike_ratio)` — Feed market data to update cascade phase
- `check_tranche(symbol: str, tranche: int, direction: str)` → `TrancheCheckResult` — Check if tranche level is allowed
- `get_status()` → `Dict` — Returns current phase, metrics, permissions, statistics
- `reset()` — Reset state machine

**Convenience Function:**
- `check_cascade_tranche(symbol, tranche, direction)` → `(allowed, reason, recommended_action)` — Simplified wrapper

---

### 2. **Input Requirements**

The governor needs these inputs for `update_metrics()`:

| Parameter | Source | Currently Available? |
|-----------|--------|---------------------|
| `liquidation_volume_1h` | Coinglass API | ✅ YES (via `coinglass_feed._last_data.liquidations`) |
| `liquidation_volume_4h` | Coinglass API | ✅ YES (computed from 24h data) |
| `price_change_1h_pct` | OHLCV bars | ❌ NO (not computed in market_data) |
| `price_change_4h_pct` | OHLCV bars | ❌ NO (referenced but never assigned) |
| `volume_spike_ratio` | market_data | ✅ YES (`market_data["volume_ratio"]`) |

**Data Gap:** Price change metrics are **referenced** at line 3012 (`market_data.get("price_change_4h_pct", 0.0)`) but **never computed**. They can be easily calculated from the OHLCV bars already available in `_prepare_market_data()`.

### 3. **Current Usage**

The governor is called from **TWO locations**:

    liquidation_volume_1h=_liq_vol_24h / 24.0,
    liquidation_volume_4h=_liq_vol_24h / 6.0,

**Issue:** `price_change_4h_pct` always defaults to 0.0, so cascade detection relies **only** on liquidation volume thresholds, not price movement.

#### **B. main.py (Tranche Guard) — Lines 5969-5990**
```python

_cascade_gov = get_cascade_exhaustion_governor()

if _cur_tranche >= 2:
    _tranche_check = _cascade_gov.check_tranche(asset, _cur_tranche, "short")
    if not _tranche_check.allowed:
        logger.warning(f"[V10A] CascadeExhaustion blocked T{_cur_tranche}: {_tranche_check.reason}")
        return {"status": "REJECTED", "reason": f"[V10A] CascadeExhaustion: {_tranche_check.reason}"}
```

**Good:** Tranche guard blocks T2+ entries during cascade phases (DETECTED/EXHAUSTING).

#### **C. defense/governor_integration.py (Wrapper) — Lines 268-303**

                          price_change_1h_pct, price_change_4h_pct, volume_spike_ratio):

            self._cascade_governor.update_metrics(...)

```

**Status:** Wrapper exists but is **not called** from main.py. Direct integration bypasses this layer.

---

### 4. **State Machine Transitions**

**Phase Flow:**
```
NONE → DETECTED → ACCELERATING → EXHAUSTING → STOP → NONE
         ↑______________|              |
                                    (rare re-accel)
```

**Transition Logic:**

| Current Phase | Next Phase | Trigger Conditions |
|---------------|------------|-------------------|
| NONE | DETECTED | `liq_vol_1h ≥ $10M` OR `price_drop_1h ≤ -3%` |
| DETECTED | ACCELERATING | `liq_vol_4h ≥ $25M` OR `price_drop_4h ≤ -7%` |
| DETECTED | EXHAUSTING | `exhaustion_signal > 0.7` |
| DETECTED | STOP | `cascade_intensity < 0.2` |
| ACCELERATING | EXHAUSTING | `exhaustion_signal > 0.5` |
| ACCELERATING | STOP | `cascade_intensity < 0.3` |
| EXHAUSTING | STOP | `exhaustion_signal > 0.8` OR `cascade_intensity < 0.1` |
| EXHAUSTING | ACCELERATING | `liq_acceleration > 0.5` (rare) |
| STOP | NONE | `cascade_intensity < 0.1` after min duration |

**Minimum Phase Duration:** 15 minutes (prevents phase ping-pong)  
**Max Cascade Duration:** 8 hours (force STOP)

**Tranche Permissions by Phase:**

| Phase | T1 | T2 | T3 | T4 |
|-------|----|----|----|----|
| NONE | ✅ | ✅ | ✅ | ✅ |
| DETECTED | ✅ | ❌ | ❌ | ❌ |
| ACCELERATING | ✅ | ✅ | ✅ | ❌ |
| EXHAUSTING | ❌ | ❌ | ❌ | ❌ |
| STOP | ✅ | ✅ | ✅ | ✅ |

---

**Current:** Already integrated at **TWO checkpoints**:

    raw['price_change_1h_pct'] = raw['price_change_4h_pct'] / 4.0  # 1H approx from 4H bar

## P6: CrossExchangeEngine (MicrostructureAgent)

  ```python
  def should_accelerate_exit(self, system_mode: str) -> Tuple[bool, float]:
      """
      CRITICAL: This is EXIT-ONLY. Does NOT block entries.
      """
      if system_mode != "OPPORTUNITY":
          return False, 0.0
      
      if len(self._correlation_buffer) < self._window_size // 2:
          return False, 0.0
      
      avg_correlation = sum(self._correlation_buffer) / len(self._correlation_buffer)
      
      if avg_correlation >= self.config.fast_correlation_exit_threshold:  # 0.90
          return True, avg_correlation
  ```

  ```

**FINDING:** **COMPLETE** - Fully implemented, exit-only (does not block entries), active only in OPPORTUNITY mode, 30min window (configurable via `fast_correlation_window_min`).

## 修复 1: Short Filter Funding Rate 阈值调整 [P0, 5分钟]

### 问题

`orchestration/strategic_coordinator.py:647`: 阈值 0.003 (0.3%) 在加密永续合约
市场太低。正常 funding 0.01-0.05%/8h，但 BTC/ETH 当前 0.33-0.50%/8h（换算
年化 ~150-220%），在极端市场并不罕见。0.3% 阈值导致 BTC/ETH 做空被永久封杀。

### 修复

**文件: `orchestration/strategic_coordinator.py`**

找到 (约 line 647):
```python
        # 4. 资金费率检查
        if funding_rate > 0.003:  # 0.3%
            result.funding_rate_extreme = True
            result.allow_new_short = False
            result.max_short_exposure = min(result.max_short_exposure, 0.3)
            result.reasons.append(
                f"Extreme funding rate ({funding_rate:.4%}) - shorts too expensive"
            )
        elif funding_rate > 0.001:  # 0.1%
            result.max_short_exposure = min(result.max_short_exposure, 0.7)
            result.reasons.append(
                f"High funding rate ({funding_rate:.4%}) - reduced short exposure"
            )
```

替换为:
```python
        # 4. 资金费率检查
        # 注意：Coinglass 返回的是每 8h 的 funding rate。
        # 正常范围: 0.01-0.05% (0.0001-0.0005)
        # 高费率:   0.05-0.30% (0.0005-0.003)
        # 极端费率: >0.30% (>0.003) — 年化 >400%
        #
        # 之前阈值 0.003 (0.3%) 太低，在高波动期封杀了所有做空。
        # Kraken spot 模式下 funding rate 是 proxy（来自其他交易所），
        # 不直接影响持仓成本，因此可以更宽容。
        if funding_rate > 0.01:  # 1.0% per 8h = 年化 ~456%
            result.funding_rate_extreme = True
            result.allow_new_short = False
            result.max_short_exposure = min(result.max_short_exposure, 0.3)
            result.reasons.append(
                f"Extreme funding rate ({funding_rate:.4%}) - shorts too expensive"
            )
        elif funding_rate > 0.005:  # 0.5% per 8h = 年化 ~228%
            result.max_short_exposure = min(result.max_short_exposure, 0.5)
            result.reasons.append(
                f"High funding rate ({funding_rate:.4%}) - reduced short exposure"
            )
        elif funding_rate > 0.003:  # 0.3% per 8h = 年化 ~137%
            result.max_short_exposure = min(result.max_short_exposure, 0.7)
            result.reasons.append(
                f"Elevated funding rate ({funding_rate:.4%}) - caution on shorts"
            )
```

### 验证
```bash
# 应该不再看到 "Extreme funding rate" 封杀 (除非 >1%)
grep "V6 SHORT FILTER.*Extreme funding" logs/*.log | tail -5
# 在 0.3-1.0% 区间，应看到 "Elevated" 而非 "Extreme"
```

---

## 修复 2: Near-Zero Partial Close 过滤 [P0, 10分钟]

### 问题

每个 tick 引擎重新评估已有仓位，产出相同的 target_exposure。
价格微变 → notional 差 $0-3 → 触发 Branch B (PARTIAL EXIT)。

**根因追踪:**
- Tick N: SOL short, entry price=$150, exposure=0.1, notional=$10000
- Tick N+1: SOL price=$150.05, engine 再次输出 target_exposure=0.1
- `_execute_intent`: base_quantity = exposure_to_quantity(0.1, $100K, $150.05) → notional_usd=$9996.7
- Paper tracker Branch B: `closed_notional = $10000 - $9996.7 = $3.3`
- 记录一笔 "$3.3 PARTIAL CLOSE"

这不是有意义的交易，是浮点数噪声。

### 修复

**文件: `main.py`**

在 Branch B (PARTIAL EXIT) 入口处加最小 notional 阈值，约 line 4280:

找到:
```python
            # =============================================================
            # BRANCH B: PARTIAL EXIT  (same direction, exposure shrinking)
            # =============================================================
            elif is_partial_exit:
                entry_price = old_pos.get("entry_price", fill_price)
                pos_dir = old_pos.get("direction", direction_sign)
                old_notional = old_pos.get("notional", 0.0)
                closed_notional = old_notional - notional_usd
```

替换为:
```python
            # =============================================================
            # BRANCH B: PARTIAL EXIT  (same direction, exposure shrinking)
            # =============================================================
            elif is_partial_exit:
                entry_price = old_pos.get("entry_price", fill_price)
                pos_dir = old_pos.get("direction", direction_sign)
                old_notional = old_pos.get("notional", 0.0)
                closed_notional = old_notional - notional_usd

                # FIX: 价格微变导致 notional 差 $0-几，不是有意义的部分平仓。
                # 过滤掉低于 $50 的 notional 变化（浮点噪声 / 价格漂移）。
                MIN_PARTIAL_CLOSE_USD = 50.0
                if abs(closed_notional) < MIN_PARTIAL_CLOSE_USD:
                    logger.debug(
                        f"[PAPER] {asset}: Skipping noise partial close "
                        f"(${closed_notional:.2f} < ${MIN_PARTIAL_CLOSE_USD})"
                    )
                    # 不更新仓位，不记录 PnL，直接跳过
                    # 注意: 不能 return，后面还有 proof log 和 feedback 代码
                    # 用 flag 跳过 PnL 记录部分
                    pass  # 下面的代码块会处理
                else:
```

**但是**，Branch B 的代码块很长（~60行），用 flag 更干净。改为:

找到完整的 Branch B:
```python
            elif is_partial_exit:
```

在其第一行后添加 early-skip:
```python
            elif is_partial_exit:
                entry_price = old_pos.get("entry_price", fill_price)
                pos_dir = old_pos.get("direction", direction_sign)
                old_notional = old_pos.get("notional", 0.0)
                closed_notional = old_notional - notional_usd

                # FIX: 过滤浮点噪声导致的 $0-几 "partial close"
                MIN_PARTIAL_CLOSE_USD = 50.0
                if abs(closed_notional) < MIN_PARTIAL_CLOSE_USD:
                    logger.debug(
                        f"[PAPER_SKIP] {asset}: noise partial ${closed_notional:.2f} "
                        f"(old=${old_notional:.0f}, new=${notional_usd:.0f})"
                    )
                    # 跳过整个 partial close — 仓位不变
                    pass
                elif closed_notional > 0:
```

**注意**: 需要确保原来 `closed_notional > 0` 之后的全部代码（PnL计算、shadow ledger、
thesis budget 记录等）被包裹在 `elif closed_notional > 0:` 里，而不是裸执行。

**更安全的做法** — 在 `is_partial_exit` 判断时就过滤:

找到 (约 line 4071):
```python
            is_partial_exit = (
                not is_full_exit
                and old_pos is not None
                and old_pos.get("direction", 0) * direction_sign > 0      # same direction
                and old_pos.get("notional", 0) > 0
                and notional_usd < old_pos.get("notional", 0)            # shrinking
            )
```

替换为:
```python
            is_partial_exit = (
                not is_full_exit
                and old_pos is not None
                and old_pos.get("direction", 0) * direction_sign > 0      # same direction
                and old_pos.get("notional", 0) > 0
                and notional_usd < old_pos.get("notional", 0)            # shrinking
                and (old_pos.get("notional", 0) - notional_usd) >= 50.0  # FIX: 过滤 $0-50 噪声
            )
```

这样 $0-50 的 notional 差异不会触发 Branch B，直接落入 Branch C（scale-in）。
但 Branch C 也不对 — 它会更新 entry_price。

**最终方案**: 在三个 branch 之后加一个 Branch D (NO-OP):

```python
            is_full_exit = (intent.target_exposure == 0 or intent.direction == 0)
            
            # FIX: 判断是否是无意义的微调（价格漂移导致 notional 差 < $50）
            is_noise_rebalance = (
                not is_full_exit
                and old_pos is not None
                and old_pos.get("notional", 0) > 0
                and abs(notional_usd - old_pos.get("notional", 0)) < 50.0
            )
            
            is_partial_exit = (
                not is_full_exit
                and not is_noise_rebalance      # ← 新增排除条件
                and old_pos is not None
                and old_pos.get("direction", 0) * direction_sign > 0
                and old_pos.get("notional", 0) > 0
                and notional_usd < old_pos.get("notional", 0)
            )

            if is_full_exit:
                # ... Branch A 不变 ...
            elif is_noise_rebalance:
                # Branch D: NO-OP — 仓位实质未变，跳过
                logger.debug(
                    f"[PAPER_NOOP] {asset}: position unchanged "
                    f"(delta=${abs(notional_usd - old_pos.get('notional', 0)):.2f})"
                )
            elif is_partial_exit:
                # ... Branch B 不变 ...
            else:
                # ... Branch C 不变 ...
```

### 验证
```bash
# 修复后不应再有 $0-几 的 partial close
grep "PARTIAL CLOSE\|PARTIAL EXIT" logs/*.log | tail -10
# 应该看到 PAPER_NOOP 日志替代了之前的 near-zero close
grep "PAPER_NOOP" logs/*.log | tail -10
```

## 修复 3: Invariant Violation 假阳性 [P1, 5分钟]

### 问题

INV-1 (`VETO_ACTIVE_BUT_NONZERO_EXPOSURE`) 对所有 veto 一视同仁。
但 V6 SHORT FILTER 和 STRUCTURE veto 是 **在 engine decide() 之后** 施加的，
此时 intent 已经有了 target_exposure。veto 阻止执行但保留 exposure 用于 proof log。
这是设计意图，不是 invariant violation。

### 修复

**文件: `main.py`**, `_check_invariants` 方法 (约 line 3728):

找到:
```python
        # INV-1: veto_active=True → target_exposure must be 0
        if intent.veto_active and abs(getattr(intent, 'target_exposure', 0)) > 0.001:
            violations.append(
                f"VETO_ACTIVE_BUT_NONZERO_EXPOSURE: "
                f"veto={intent.veto_reason}, exposure={intent.target_exposure}"
            )
```

替换为:
```python
        # INV-1: veto_active=True → target_exposure must be 0
        # 排除: 某些 governor 在 engine 产出 exposure 之后施加 veto，
        # 此时保留 exposure 用于 proof log 是正确行为。
        _POST_DECIDE_VETOES = {
            "V6 SHORT FILTER",      # 在 decide() 后施加
            "STRUCTURE",            # 结构检查
            "THESIS_BUDGET",        # 预算控制
            "VOLUME_CONTRACTING",   # 成交量收缩
            "TRADE_GATE",           # 交易门控
            "P0_SAFETY",            # P0 安全检查
            "P0 SHORT BLOCK",       # P0 做空限制
            "RISK_GOVERNOR",        # 风控限制
        }
        veto_reason = getattr(intent, 'veto_reason', '') or ''
        is_post_decide_veto = any(tag in veto_reason for tag in _POST_DECIDE_VETOES)
        
        if intent.veto_active and abs(getattr(intent, 'target_exposure', 0)) > 0.001:
            if not is_post_decide_veto:
                violations.append(
                    f"VETO_ACTIVE_BUT_NONZERO_EXPOSURE: "
                    f"veto={intent.veto_reason}, exposure={intent.target_exposure}"
                )
            # 对 post-decide veto 只记 debug，不报 CRITICAL
```

### 验证
```bash
# 修复后 INVARIANT VIOLATION 数量应大幅减少
grep "INVARIANT VIOLATION" logs/*.log | wc -l
# 应该接近 0（除非有真正的 invariant violation）
```

---

## 修复 4: MAX_HOLD_TIMEOUT 做空方向 Bug [P0, 5分钟]

### 问题

这是已知 Bug 1 (PRE_LIVE_CRITICAL_FIXES.md) 的补充发现。

MAX_HOLD 用 `market_data.get("current_exposure", 0.0)` 判断方向，
但 paper_positions 存储 exposure 为 **正值** + 独立 direction 字段。
因此 `current_exposure` 永远 > 0，`exit_direction` 永远 = -1 (SELL)。

做多仓位: SELL → 正确
**做空仓位: SELL → 错误!** 应该 BUY (+1) 才能平空。

### 修复

已在 PRE_LIVE_CRITICAL_FIXES.md 中用 `target_exposure=0` + inline execution 解决。
但如果保留 direction-based exit，需要修正方向逻辑:

在 Bug 1 的修复中，exit intent 改用了 `target_exposure=0.0`，
此时 direction 不影响 Branch A (FULL EXIT)。但为安全起见，direction 也应正确:

```python
                    # FIX: 用 position_direction 而非 exposure 符号判断退出方向
                    pos_dir = market_data.get("position_direction", 0)
                    if pos_dir > 0:
                        exit_direction = -1.0   # 平多 → SELL
                    elif pos_dir < 0:
                        exit_direction = 1.0    # 平空 → BUY
                    else:
                        # 无方向信息，用旧逻辑兜底
                        exit_direction = -1.0 if current_exposure > 0 else 1.0
```

---

## 修复 5: Invariant 假阳性 补充 — profit_max_extreme_funding_threshold [P2, 2分钟]

### 问题

`main.py:753`: `profit_max_extreme_funding_threshold: float = 0.15` (15%)
这个阈值和 Short Filter 的 0.3% 完全不在一个量级。
ProfitMax 用 0.15 (15%) 作为极端 funding，而 Short Filter 用 0.003 (0.3%)。

### 注意

这两个阈值的 **单位不同**。确认:
- ProfitMax: 可能是年化 funding rate (15% annualized)?
- Short Filter: 是每 8h 的原始 funding rate

如果单位确实不同，只需加注释说明。如果单位相同，ProfitMax 的 15% 太高。

```bash
# 确认 profit_max 如何使用这个阈值:
grep -B5 -A10 "extreme_funding_threshold" main.py | head -20
```

## Task 1: orderbook_depth NaN Guard [P1, 10分钟]

### 问题
`main.py` ~line 5399: `orderbook_depth_1pct = float("inf")` 作为默认值。
~line 5905: `_depth_buf.append(depth_1pct)` 把 inf 放入 rolling buffer。
~line 5911: `median([inf, inf, inf]) = inf` → `inf / inf = NaN`。
NaN 到了 v36 engine 的 `validate_market_data()` → 整个 tick 被杀。
每次发生就丢失一个 4h 交易窗口。已观察到 9 次。

### 修复

**文件: `main.py`**, 约 line 5905

找到:
```python
        if _depth_buf is not None and depth_1pct > 0:
            _depth_buf.append(depth_1pct)
```

替换为:
```python
        if _depth_buf is not None and depth_1pct > 0 and depth_1pct < float('inf'):
            _depth_buf.append(depth_1pct)
```

找到 (~line 5911):
```python
        raw['orderbook_depth'] = min(depth_1pct / _depth_median, 5.0) if _depth_median > 0 else 1.0
```

替换为:
```python
        import math
        _raw_depth = min(depth_1pct / _depth_median, 5.0) if (_depth_median > 0 and depth_1pct < float('inf')) else 1.0
        raw['orderbook_depth'] = _raw_depth if (not math.isnan(_raw_depth) and not math.isinf(_raw_depth)) else 1.0
```

### 验证
```bash
python3 -c "
# 模拟 inf 场景
import math
depth_1pct = float('inf')
_depth_median = float('inf')
_raw_depth = min(depth_1pct / _depth_median, 5.0) if (_depth_median > 0 and depth_1pct < float('inf')) else 1.0
result = _raw_depth if (not math.isnan(_raw_depth) and not math.isinf(_raw_depth)) else 1.0
assert result == 1.0, f'Expected 1.0, got {result}'
print('NaN guard test PASSED')
"
```

## Task 2: Shadow Ledger Direction 修复 [P1, 5分钟]

### 问题
`main.py` ~line 3513: `record_position_change()` 调用时不传 `old_direction` 
和 `new_direction`，默认值全是 0。shadow ledger 审计日志无法区分多空。

### 修复

**文件: `main.py`**, 约 line 3505-3520

找到:
```python
            try:
                old_position = position_state.get("current_exposure", 0)
                new_position = old_position + (intent.target_exposure * intent.direction)
                
                self.p0_integrator.record_position_change(
                    asset=asset,
                    old_position=old_position,
                    new_position=new_position,
                    reason=f"Intent: dir={intent.direction}, exp={intent.target_exposure:.4f}, "
                           f"veto={intent.veto_active}, reason={intent.veto_reason or 'none'}"
                )
```

替换为:
```python
            try:
                old_position = position_state.get("current_exposure", 0)
                new_position = old_position + (intent.target_exposure * intent.direction)
                
                # FIX: 传递实际方向到 shadow ledger
                old_dir = int(position_state.get("position_direction", 0))
                new_dir = int(1 if intent.direction > 0 else (-1 if intent.direction < 0 else 0))
                if intent.target_exposure == 0:
                    new_dir = 0  # 平仓
                
                self.p0_integrator.record_position_change(
                    asset=asset,
                    old_position=old_position,
                    new_position=new_position,
                    old_direction=old_dir,
                    new_direction=new_dir,
                    reason=f"Intent: dir={intent.direction}, exp={intent.target_exposure:.4f}, "
                           f"veto={intent.veto_active}, reason={intent.veto_reason or 'none'}"
                )
```

### 验证
```bash

# 确认函数签名有 old_direction 和 new_direction 参数

## Task 3: NAV/Drawdown 周期性日志 [P1, 15分钟]

### 问题
没有 `[NAV]` 或 `[DRAWDOWN]` 日志。`current_drawdown` 在 paper 模式
硬编码为 0.0 (`main.py` ~line 5468)。无法追踪权益曲线或回撤。

### 修复 A: 添加 NAV 快照

**文件: `main.py`**, 在 `run_paper()` 中，`self._save_paper_positions()` 之后
(约 line 4871)，添加:

```python
                # ========================================================
                # NAV/Drawdown 快照 (每 round 一次)
                # ========================================================
                try:
                    current_equity = 100_000.0
                    if self.account_sync:
                        try:
                            current_equity = self.account_sync.get_equity()
                        except Exception:
                            pass
                    
                    if not hasattr(self, '_peak_equity'):
                        self._peak_equity = current_equity
                    self._peak_equity = max(self._peak_equity, current_equity)
                    
                    drawdown_pct = 0.0
                    if self._peak_equity > 0:
                        drawdown_pct = (self._peak_equity - current_equity) / self._peak_equity
                    
                    # 保存供 _fetch_live_data 使用
                    self._current_drawdown_pct = drawdown_pct
                    
                    logger.info(
                        f"[NAV] equity=${current_equity:,.2f} | "
                        f"peak=${self._peak_equity:,.2f} | "
                        f"drawdown={drawdown_pct:.2%} | "
                        f"positions={len(self._paper_positions)} | "
                        f"round={round_count}"
                    )
                    
                    if drawdown_pct >= self.config.hard_drawdown_halt:
                        logger.critical(
                            f"[DRAWDOWN_HALT] {drawdown_pct:.2%} >= "
                            f"{self.config.hard_drawdown_halt:.2%} — STOPPING"
                        )
                        self._running = False
                except Exception as e:
                    logger.debug(f"[NAV] Snapshot failed: {e}")
```

### 修复 B: 修复 current_drawdown 硬编码

**文件: `main.py`**, 约 line 5468

找到:
```python
                "current_drawdown": 0.0,
```

替换为:
```python
                "current_drawdown": getattr(self, '_current_drawdown_pct', 0.0),
```

### 验证
```bash

# [NAV] equity=$100,531.00 | peak=$100,800.00 | drawdown=0.27% | positions=2 | round=5
grep "[NAV]" logs/*.log | tail -3
```

## Task 5: S-04 Cascade Governor 持久化 [P1, 20分钟]

### 问题
`risk/cascade_exhaustion_governor.py` (339行) 有完整的状态管理，
但重启后状态丢失。cascade 的 phase (DETECTING→ACTIVE→EXHAUSTION→RECOVERY)
在重启时重置为 NONE。如果重启时正处于 CASCADE_ACTIVE 期间，
重启后会错误地允许新开仓。

### 修复

Cascade governor 没有 `to_dict/from_dict` 用于自身实例。
(现有的 `to_dict` 是在 CascadeSnapshot 和 TrancheCheckResult 上，不是 governor 本身。)

**文件: `risk/cascade_exhaustion_governor.py`**, 在 `CascadeExhaustionGovernor` 类末尾添加:

```python
    def to_dict(self) -> Dict:
        """Serialize governor state for persistence."""
        return {
            "phase": self._current_phase.value,
            "cascade_start": self._cascade_start.isoformat() if self._cascade_start else None,
            "last_snapshot": self._last_snapshot.to_dict() if self._last_snapshot else None,
            "cooldown_until": self._cooldown_until.isoformat() if self._cooldown_until else None,
        }
    
    def from_dict(self, data: Dict):
        """Restore governor state from persistence."""
        from datetime import datetime
        phase_str = data.get("phase", "NONE")
        try:
            self._current_phase = CascadePhase(phase_str)
        except (ValueError, KeyError):
            self._current_phase = CascadePhase.NONE
        
        if data.get("cascade_start"):
            self._cascade_start = datetime.fromisoformat(data["cascade_start"])
        
        if data.get("cooldown_until"):
            self._cooldown_until = datetime.fromisoformat(data["cooldown_until"])
        
        logger.info(
            f"[CASCADE] State restored: phase={self._current_phase.value}, "
            f"cascade_start={self._cascade_start}"
        )
```

**文件: `main.py`**, 在 `_save_paper_positions()` 的 payload 中添加:

```python
            # Cascade governor state
            cascade_state = {}
            if hasattr(self, 'cascade_governor') and self.cascade_governor:
                try:
                    cascade_state = self.cascade_governor.to_dict()
                except Exception as e:
                    logger.debug(f"[PAPER] Cascade state capture failed: {e}")
```

将 `cascade_state` 添加到 payload dict。

**文件: `main.py`**, 在 `_load_paper_positions()` 中添加恢复:

```python
            # Restore cascade governor
            cascade_data = data.get("cascade_state", {})
            if cascade_data and hasattr(self, 'cascade_governor') and self.cascade_governor:
                try:
                    self.cascade_governor.from_dict(cascade_data)
                except Exception as e:
                    logger.warning(f"[PAPER] Cascade restore failed: {e}")
```

### 验证
```bash
# 运行一个 tick 后检查:
cat data/paper_positions.json | python3 -c "
import sys, json
d = json.load(sys.stdin)
print('cascade_state:', json.dumps(d.get('cascade_state', 'MISSING'), indent=2))
"
```

### 现状
