## Task 1: PartialConsensusChecker SHADOW 接入 [P2, 1小时]

### 背景
`signals/partial_consensus.py` (534行) 完整实现。允许在部分共识条件下
以 30-50% 减仓开 T1。当前从未被调用。

关键API:
```python
class PartialConsensusChecker:
    def check(
        self,
        decider_agent: str,          # "quant"
        decider_direction: float,     # agent_signals['quant_direction']
        decider_confidence: float,    # agent_signals['quant_confidence'] 
        signals: Dict[str, Any],      # {agent_name: AgentSignal(direction, confidence)}
        authority_matrix: Dict,       # {agent_name: Authority}
        disable_conditions: Optional[DisableConditions] = None,
    ) -> PartialConsensusResult
```

`configs/sota_flags.py:149`: `ENABLE_PARTIAL_CONSENSUS_ENTRY: bool = False`
先保持 False（SHADOW 模式），只记录"如果启用，会怎样"。

### 修复

#### Step A: 初始化

**文件: `main.py`**, 在初始化区域（其他 defense/signal 组件初始化附近），添加:

```python
        # PartialConsensusChecker — SHADOW mode (log-only)
        self._partial_consensus = None
        try:
            from signals.partial_consensus import PartialConsensusChecker
            self._partial_consensus = PartialConsensusChecker()
            # Force SHADOW: enabled=False means check() returns immediately,
            # so we need a separate shadow flag
            self._partial_consensus_shadow = True
            self._partial_consensus.enabled = True  # Enable logic but don't act on results
            logger.info("  PartialConsensusChecker: SHADOW (log-only)")
        except Exception as e:
            logger.warning(f"  PartialConsensusChecker: UNAVAILABLE ({e})")
```

#### Step B: 在 veto 链中调用

**文件: `main.py`**, 在 engine.decide() 返回 intent 之后，governor chain 之前
（约 line 2790+，在 tranche persist 之后），添加:

```python
        # =================================================================
        # SHADOW: PartialConsensusChecker — 记录潜在的部分共识机会
        # 只在 intent 被 signal conflict 或 no consensus veto 时检查
        # =================================================================
        if (self._partial_consensus is not None 
            and intent.veto_active 
            and any(tag in (intent.veto_reason or '') for tag in ['CONFLICT', 'NO_CONSENSUS', 'ALPHA_GATE'])):
            try:
                from signals.partial_consensus import DisableConditions
                
                # 构建 agent signals 格式
                class _PCSignal:
                    def __init__(self, d, c):
                        self.direction = d
                        self.confidence = c
                
                class _PCAuthority:
                    def __init__(self, v):
                        self.value = v
                
                pc_signals = {
                    'quant': _PCSignal(
                        agent_signals.get('quant_direction', 0),
                        agent_signals.get('quant_confidence', 0)),
                    'sentiment': _PCSignal(
                        agent_signals.get('sentiment_direction', 0),
                        agent_signals.get('sentiment_confidence', 0)),
                    'regime': _PCSignal(
                        agent_signals.get('regime_direction', 0),
                        agent_signals.get('regime_confidence', 0)),
                }
                
                pc_authority = {
                    'quant': _PCAuthority('DECIDE'),
                    'sentiment': _PCAuthority('CONFIRM'),
                    'regime': _PCAuthority('CONFIRM'),
                }
                
                # Disable conditions from current state
                pc_disable = DisableConditions(
                    correlation_cap_level=agent_signals.get('correlation_cap_level', 'NORMAL'),
                    cascade_phase=agent_signals.get('cascade_phase', 'NONE'),
                    dvol_zscore=market_data.get('dvol_zscore', 0.0),
                )
                
                pc_result = self._partial_consensus.check(
                    decider_agent='quant',
                    decider_direction=agent_signals.get('quant_direction', 0),
                    decider_confidence=agent_signals.get('quant_confidence', 0),
                    signals=pc_signals,
                    authority_matrix=pc_authority,
                    disable_conditions=pc_disable,
                )
                
                if pc_result.is_partial_consensus:
                    logger.info(
                        f"[PARTIAL_CONSENSUS_SHADOW] {asset}: WOULD allow reduced entry — "
                        f"scale={pc_result.scale_factor:.2f}, "
                        f"agreeing={pc_result.agreeing_agents}, "
                        f"original_veto={intent.veto_reason}"
                    )
                else:
                    logger.debug(
                        f"[PARTIAL_CONSENSUS_SHADOW] {asset}: no partial consensus — "
                        f"reason={pc_result.reason}"
                    )
            except Exception as e:
                logger.debug(f"[PARTIAL_CONSENSUS_SHADOW] {asset}: check failed: {e}")
```

### 验证
```bash
grep "PARTIAL_CONSENSUS_SHADOW" logs/*.log | tail -10
# SHADOW 期间只看 log，不影响任何交易决策
```

### 毕业条件（未来启用时）
观察 1-2 周 SHADOW 数据后，如果 WOULD allow 的频率合理（<20% of vetoed ticks），
在 `configs/sota_flags.py` 设置 `ENABLE_PARTIAL_CONSENSUS_ENTRY = True`，
并将 SHADOW 代码改为实际修改 intent（解除 veto + 缩小 exposure）。

## Task 2: DataDriftDetector SHADOW 接入 [P2, 1小时]

### 背景
`defense/drift_detector.py` (586行) 完整实现。监控 feature 分布漂移，
自动调整 DRL 权重。当前已 import (`main.py:159`) 但未实例化。

关键API:
```python
class DataDriftDetector:
    def add_features(self, features: Dict[str, float])    # 喂入特征
    def check_all_drift(self) -> (DriftSeverity, details)  # 检查漂移
    def get_drl_weight_multiplier(self) -> float           # 获取 DRL 权重调整
```

### 修复

#### Step A: 初始化

**文件: `main.py`**, 在初始化区域添加:

```python
        # DataDriftDetector — SHADOW mode (log-only)
        self._drift_detector = None
        self._drift_detector_shadow = True  # SHADOW: don't adjust DRL weight
        if DEFENSE_AVAILABLE:
            try:
                self._drift_detector = DataDriftDetector()
                logger.info("  DataDriftDetector: SHADOW (log-only)")
            except Exception as e:
                logger.warning(f"  DataDriftDetector: UNAVAILABLE ({e})")
```

#### Step B: 在每个 tick 中喂入特征 + 检测

**文件: `main.py`**, 在 data enrichment 完成之后、engine.decide() 之前
（约 line 2530 附近，lead-lag 计算之后），添加:

```python
        # =================================================================
        # SHADOW: DataDriftDetector — 监控特征分布漂移
        # =================================================================
        if self._drift_detector is not None:
            try:
                # 喂入关键特征（与 DRL observation space 对齐）
                drift_features = {
                    'rsi': market_data.get('rsi', 50.0),
                    'adx': market_data.get('adx', 25.0),
                    'volatility_4h': market_data.get('volatility_4h', 0.0),
                    'volume_ratio': market_data.get('volume_ratio', 1.0),
                    'spread_bps': market_data.get('spread_bps', 10.0),
                    'dvol_zscore': market_data.get('dvol_zscore', 0.0),
                    'vpin': market_data.get('vpin', 0.35),
                    'cross_asset_correlation': market_data.get('cross_asset_correlation', 0.87),
                    'orderbook_depth': market_data.get('orderbook_depth', 1.0),
                    'funding_rate': market_data.get('funding_rate', 0.0),
                }
                self._drift_detector.add_features(drift_features)
                
                # 检查漂移（内部有 rate limit，不会每 tick 都检查）
                severity, details = self._drift_detector.check_all_drift()
                
                from defense.drift_detector import DriftSeverity
                if severity != DriftSeverity.NONE:
                    drl_mult = self._drift_detector.get_drl_weight_multiplier()
                    logger.warning(
                        f"[DATA_DRIFT_SHADOW] {asset}: severity={severity.name}, "
                        f"drl_weight_mult={drl_mult:.2f}, "
                        f"drifted_features={[k for k,v in details.get('features',{}).items() if v.get('drift')]}"
                    )
                    
                    # SHADOW: 记录但不应用 DRL weight adjustment
                    # 启用时: agent_signals['drl_drift_weight'] = drl_mult
                else:
                    logger.debug(f"[DATA_DRIFT_SHADOW] {asset}: no drift detected")
                    
            except Exception as e:
                logger.debug(f"[DATA_DRIFT_SHADOW] {asset}: check failed: {e}")
```

### 验证
```bash
grep "DATA_DRIFT_SHADOW" logs/*.log | tail -10
# 前 ~1000 个 tick 不会有漂移（reference window 还在建立）
# 之后如果市场分布变化，会看到漂移事件
```

### 毕业条件
当 DRL 从 DISABLED 升级到 EXIT_ONLY/SHADOW 后，启用 drift detector
实际调整 `agent_signals['drl_drift_weight']`，防止 DRL 在 OOD 分布上
做出错误决策。

## Task 3: Lead-Lag 噪声阈值调整 [P2, 5分钟]

### 背景
`main.py:2571-2572`:
```python
if abs(_ll_edge_bps) < 5.0:
    _ll_conf *= 0.3
```

当前 89% QUIET_ACCUMULATION → BTC/ETH/SOL 同步移动 → edge < 5bps → 
confidence 被压到 0.3× → lead-lag 对下游决策几乎无影响。

降低阈值到 2bps 可以让 lead-lag 在安静市场也产生微弱信号。
这不影响风控（lead-lag 只是加分项，不直接开仓），风险极低。

### 修复

**文件: `main.py`**, 约 line 2571

找到:
```python
                    # Dampen if edge is tiny (<5 bps) — noise, not signal
                    if abs(_ll_edge_bps) < 5.0:
                        _ll_conf *= 0.3
```

替换为:
```python
                    # Dampen if edge is tiny — noise, not signal
                    # v5.10.1: lowered from 5.0→2.0 bps to allow weak signals
                    # in quiet markets (89% QUIET_ACCUMULATION was zeroing out all lead-lag)
                    if abs(_ll_edge_bps) < 2.0:
                        _ll_conf *= 0.1   # Very tiny edge: heavily dampen
                    elif abs(_ll_edge_bps) < 5.0:
                        _ll_conf *= 0.5   # Small edge: moderate dampen
```

### 验证
```bash
# 修复后应该看到更多 lead-lag 日志（>=2bps 就有 log）
grep "LEAD_LAG" logs/*.log | tail -10
# 之前: 几乎没有 (全被 5bps 过滤)
# 之后: 应该有 2-5bps 区间的信号出现
```

2. Key Technical Concepts:

   - **`risk/cascade_exhaustion_governor.py`** (after L534, before SINGLETON section)
     - Added `to_dict()`/`from_dict()` methods to `CascadeExhaustionGovernor` class.
     ```python
     def to_dict(self) -> Dict:
         return {
             "phase": self._phase.value,
             "phase_start": self._phase_start.isoformat() if self._phase_start else None,
             "cascade_start": self._cascade_start.isoformat() if self._cascade_start else None,
             "blocks_count": self._blocks_count,
             "allows_count": self._allows_count,
             "phase_transitions": self._phase_transitions[-20:],
         }

     def from_dict(self, data: Dict):
         phase_str = data.get("phase", "NONE")
         try:
             self._phase = CascadePhase(phase_str)
         except (ValueError, KeyError):
             self._phase = CascadePhase.NONE
         if data.get("phase_start"):
             try:
                 self._phase_start = datetime.fromisoformat(data["phase_start"])
             except (ValueError, TypeError):
                 pass
         if data.get("cascade_start"):
             try:
                 self._cascade_start = datetime.fromisoformat(data["cascade_start"])
             except (ValueError, TypeError):
                 pass
         self._blocks_count = data.get("blocks_count", 0)
         self._allows_count = data.get("allows_count", 0)
         self._phase_transitions = data.get("phase_transitions", [])
         logger.info(f"[CASCADE] State restored: phase={self._phase.value}, blocks={self._blocks_count}, allows={self._allows_count}")
     ```

4. Errors and Fixes:
   - **cascade_state MISSING in paper_positions.json**: After first restart, `cascade_state` was empty/missing. Root cause: save code used `hasattr(self, 'cascade_governor')` but the cascade governor is NOT a `self.*` instance attribute — it's accessed via module-level singleton `get_cascade_exhaustion_governor()`. Fixed by changing save to call `from risk.cascade_exhaustion_governor import get_cascade_exhaustion_governor; _ceg = get_cascade_exhaustion_governor(); cascade_state = _ceg.to_dict()` and same pattern for restore. Required a second restart.
   
   - **PowerShell `$_.ProcessId` expansion by bash**: `Get-CimInstance Win32_Process | Where-Object { $_.ProcessId -in @(...) }` failed because bash expanded `$_`. Fixed by using single-quoted PowerShell strings: `powershell -Command 'Get-Process -Id 394784,88660 ...'`.
   
   - **Tasks 1, 2, 3 already implemented**: User's fix plan included orderbook_depth NaN guard, shadow ledger direction, and NAV/drawdown logging as tasks, but code inspection showed all three were already present in the codebase (from prior work sessions). Reported as already-done rather than making duplicate changes.
   
   - **DataDriftDetector (P2 Task 2) already wired**: User's plan called for SHADOW integration, but the detector was already fully wired at L2378-2384 (init) and L4465-4535 (tick processing with drift severity → DRL weight adjustment → auto-demotion). Gated by `_drl_authority_level not in ("DISABLED",)` so auto-activates when DRL promotes.

Used the existing `_save_paper_positions`/`_load_paper_positions` payload extension pattern (same as existence_fuse). Required adding `to_dict()`/`from_dict()` to 4 classes that lacked them.

This was critical for correct save/load wiring.
